- **2026-09-27** — **Handwritten thank-you notes can be photographed onto a testimonial — internally (LIVE).** Office is entering a year of handwritten cards as reviews, and the obvious move (photograph the card, upload it) would have **published** it: `field_testimonial_image` renders on `/about-us/reviews`, and a handwritten note carries a **signature** and often a name, address or phone number the customer never agreed to put on a website. An image of text is also invisible to Google and to a screen reader, so it makes a poor review even setting privacy aside. New **`field_testimonial_scan`** (image, **multi-value** — notes run onto a second page) holds the photo as the record of where the quote came from; the **typed quote in `field_testimony` stays the public artifact**. `field_testimonial_image` keeps its job — a note worth showing off can be **cropped and placed there deliberately**. **Access enforced in code**, not by leaving the field off a display: `bos_testimonial_entity_field_access()` (VIEW only, `user.roles` cache context) forbids non-office on `field_testimonial_scan` **and `field_submitter_email`**, which had the same exposure — Views, JSON:API and REST each render fields by their own rules, so a display that happens not to list a field is presentation, not access. Unlike the credential licence number, these are **never** public on any record, so the guard **fails closed with no itemless exception**. **Verified by rendering** the public view as anon with an approved record carrying both fields: quote renders, neither private value appears, office sees both. _The first attempt proved nothing_ — the presave backstop had forced the test record to `pending`, so the view had **zero rows** and "no leak" was vacuous; creating it as office made it a real test. Live checked **read-only** (no test data created there): matrix anon/client/teammate=deny, office=allow; `/about-us/reviews` 200, renders, 0 scan paths. Script `setup_testimonial_scan_field.php` (idempotent, per-env — ECK/field configs skip cim), verifier `verify_testimonial_field_access.php`. Commit `0e2aa58b`. **Possible follow-up (not built):** BOS already runs Claude vision for the invoice-photo importer (`wo_material_list_management.invoice_vision`), so a "read the note" button could transcribe the handwriting into `field_testimony` for office to check — same stack, no new dependency.

- **2026-09-27** — **About Us now reaches Credentials + Reviews three ways; visitor-facing PHP errors hidden; footer identity column centered on landscape/tablet (LIVE).** (1) **Nav:** `/about-us/credentials` and `/about-us/reviews` were already URL children of About but reachable only by typing the address (Credentials also via the footer trust line). Added them as **children of "About" in the main menu** — which is what produces Olivero's `+`/`-` toggle; the nav block is already `depth: 2`, so no block change — and as labelled links in the **footer Company menu** directly under About Us (Careers/Contact/Estimate/Login reweighted down). (2) **In-text:** the About copy itself now links at the moments the question occurs — "Licensed and insured" (hero **and** closing trust lines) and "outside **our licensing**" in *What We Do* point at Credentials; a closing paragraph in *Practical Advice* invites the review. (3) **Footer bug:** on landscape phones and tablets the identity column (logo/NAP/`LICENSED, BONDED & INSURED`) was stranded in the right-hand column with dead space beside it — below `64rem` it is meant to span the two-column band and center. With no browser available to measure, the fix does **not** bet on one cause: it pins **both** the full-row span (`grid-column: 1 / -1` on promo/company/servicearea) **and** the centering (`justify-self: center` + `width:100%` + `max-width:18rem`), so it holds whether the named grid area fails to resolve or `margin-inline: auto` loses to the item's stretch; no effect where the areas already place it correctly. **Band note:** every viewport ≤1024px CSS gets the two-column footer, so tablet portrait *and* most tablet landscape were affected and are covered; iPad-Pro-landscape (1366) uses the 4-column desktop footer where the column is narrow and already centered. (4) **Logging:** anonymous visitors were being shown PHP warnings with full backtraces (`system.logging error_level: all`). Live set to **`hide`**; **dblog still captures everything** (106 rows), so nothing is lost for diagnosis. `error_level` is environment-specific — dev keeps `all` — and `config/sync` carries `all`, so **`system.logging` was added to `config_ignore`** alongside s3fs / stage_file_proxy / the key entities / the geocoder provider, or a future partial-cim would put customer-visible traces back. Menu links + body copy are **content** → idempotent per-env scripts (`add_about_trust_links.php`, `link_about_trust_pages.php`; the copy edits skip once linked and report a **miss** rather than guessing if wording changed). Deploy: rsync `footer.css` + scp 2 scripts + run + `cr` (no cim/DB). Verified live anon: `/about-us` 200 with 3 in-text credential links + the review invitation, About nav renders the toggle and both children, footer Company menu carries both, both child pages 200, served `footer.css` byte-identical to local. Commits `e6fbaba6`, `cb2ad86b`. **Note:** `/about-us/reviews` has **no approved reviews yet** — the menu item currently lands on the empty state plus the leave-a-review CTA; worth approving a few (or holding that one menu item) before it gets traffic.

- **2026-09-28** — **Reviews split into office + public surfaces, and an admin-menu nesting bug fixed (LIVE).** **(1) The menu bug, which was mine:** the Credentials and Reviews menu links were created with **`parent: ''`**, which puts a Views menu link at the **ROOT of the admin menu** — Drupal nests the admin menu by **parent, not by path**, so a correct URL does not imply a correct menu position. Every working sibling sets it explicitly (`city_administration` → `menu_link_content:<Service Areas>`). Fixed by `fix_admin_menu_parents.php`, which resolves the container **by URI at runtime** rather than hardcoding a UUID (menu_link_content is content, so UUIDs differ per environment): **Credentials → Operations ▸ System Content**, **Reviews → Office**. Verified in the rendered menu tree. _(Also clarified: `admin/operations/site/system_content` has no route and no such menu item — the real container is `/admin/operations/system_content`, which is where Credentials already lived; only the nesting was wrong.)_ **(2) Reviews now has four surfaces, with distinct voices:** `/about-us/reviews` **PUBLIC** (approved only, customer voice, "Leave a review" CTA, its own metatags — sitting beside `/about-us/credentials` as the other "here is the evidence" page) · `/admin/office/reviews` office home, all statuses, the **Reviews** link under Office · `…/pending` tab · `…/approved` tab (quote cards, office voice, "use these for marketing copy"). The public page had been retired an hour earlier when Todd asked for the office URL; he then noted the display side still mattered, so it came back at the new path. Verified **16/16** by rendering every display: anonymous gets **zero** rows from all three office surfaces, and on the public page the approved review shows while the **pending one stays hidden** — the filter uses operator `or` because `in` emits no SQL on a list_string filter and would publish unreviewed text. **Recorded on the ROADMAP, deliberately not built:** Todd's plan for a **home-page banner** and a **scrolling footer strip** of reviews, gated on having approved records (0 today).

- **2026-09-28** — **`/reviews` public page + trust line on the campaign footers (LIVE).** Two gaps Todd spotted immediately after the review-capture work. **(1) There was nowhere to READ reviews** — capture (`/review`) and office moderation shipped, but the display gap was only *flagged*, not closed. `/reviews` now renders **approved** testimonials as quote cards (orange left accent, Oswald attribution, optional photo), newest first, 30 per page, with a "leave a review" CTA in the header and footer and an **honest empty state** so the page could publish ahead of the first approval — which is its current state, since there are 0 approved records. Metatags: "Customer Reviews | Brookstone Outdoors". **The filter is `field_status = approved` with operator `or`, and on this page a wrong operator is a CONTENT-SAFETY bug, not a cosmetic one** — `in` emits no SQL on a list_string ManyToOne filter, which would publish unreviewed text from strangers. Verified as **anon** with one approved + one pending record: the approved one renders, the pending one and its text are absent (7/7). **(2) The trust line was missing from the campaign landing pages** — `/winterize`, `/winterize/week`, `/winterize/bear-creek` and fall-cleanup render a **bespoke `bo-footer`** and deliberately none of the site regions, so they never inherited the sitewide footer's line, and those are the conversion pages where "Licensed, Bonded & Insured" earns most. Passed through as **`bo_trust`** from the **same `field_fc_credentials` field** rather than hardcoded (one office edit still changes every surface), wrapped in a link to `/about-us/credentials`, with a helper that swallows failure and returns `''` so a missing block can never break a landing page. Verified live: `/reviews` + `/review` + `/about-us` carry the **sitewide** line, the three campaign pages carry the **campaign** line, all five 200, every one linking to the credentials page. **Also recorded for next time:** the sitewide footer renders only in `brookstone_olivero`, so it is correctly absent on admin-themed pages — the likely reason it looked missing while browsing as office.

- **2026-09-28** — **Customer review capture — `/review` page + QR + testimonial moderation (LIVE, new `bos_testimonial`).** Todd asked whether a QR could lead to a page that enters a review into **both** Google and the internal `testimonial` entity. **It cannot, and the reason is Google's:** there is **no API for submitting a review** — the Business Profile API reads reviews and posts replies, but cannot create one; a review must be typed by the customer while signed into their own Google account on Google's surface. So no BOS form can capture review text and forward it. **Second constraint, deliberately respected:** sentiment-based routing (asking how it went, then sending happy customers to Google and unhappy ones to a private form) is **review gating**, which breaches Google's prohibited-content policy and gets reviews removed — so the page offers **both paths to every visitor with no branching question**. Built: **`/review`** — Google CTA first (Todd's shortlink `g.page/r/CUMmZtdVNzoaEBI/review` + his wording, both in `bos_testimonial.settings`; an empty URL hides the button rather than rendering a dead CTA), a divider, then a BOS testimonial form (reCAPTCHA + 5/hr per-IP flood). **Moderation came first, because the entity had none:** `testimonial` shipped with 5 content fields and **no approval flag**, so a public form would have written straight in with nothing marking a submission unreviewed. Added **`field_status`** (pending/approved/rejected, default **pending**), `field_work_order` (attribution) and `field_submitter_email` (never published) — and a **presave backstop** that forces `pending` for any non-office creator, so a crafted POST cannot arrive approved (**verified**: an `approved` submission from anonymous lands `pending`). **QR** at `/admin/operations/system_content/testimonials/qr` — same endroid `Builder` call as `ServiceRequestQrController`, inline or `?download=1` for the print shop; **generic, or `?wo=NNN` for a per-job code** so a review is attached to the job it is about (print on completion paperwork — the thing a generic review card cannot do). **Moderation list** at `/admin/operations/system_content/testimonials` with a **Pending** tab, wired `menu = admin` / `type = normal` like its siblings under that path; the pending filter uses operator **`or`** (a list_string filter is ManyToOne and has **no `in` operator`**). Verified dev **7/7 by RENDERING** every display with real rows as an office user plus both QR PNG variants; live: `/review` **200** with the Google link, form and captcha and **0** gating language, `?wo=` variant 200, all three admin paths **403** to anon. **Note:** the first live check showed the view paths 404 — `cr` had not yet run in the deploy session; a rebuild turned them into the expected 403. **Remaining (design call, not built):** nothing displays approved testimonials publicly yet — no view existed before today and a placement (homepage band / service pages / dedicated page) was deliberately not invented.

The template wraps it in a link to **`/about-us/credentials`**, so the claim sits one click from the records that substantiate it. Styled non-italic, letter-spaced, uppercase at 0.875rem so it reads as a fact rather than a slogan (the italic tagline above it stays the slogan). Verified live anon on `/`, `/about-us` and a service page: trust line present on all, linked, `footer.css` 200. Scripts: `setup_footer_credentials_line.php` (idempotent; never overwrites an existing value). **⚠ Flagged to Todd, not resolved by Code:** **"Bonded" is not substantiated by anything in BOS** — none of the 10 `credential_types` is a surety or licence bond and none of the 8 seeded records is one, so a visitor following the link sees licences and insurance but nothing bonded. "Licensed" and "Insured" are both backed. Options given: add a *Surety Bond* credential type if the company does carry one (making it verifiable like the rest), or reword to "Licensed & Insured" — a ten-second field edit, no deploy. Text was placed exactly as requested; the flag is on record because the credentials page is built on the principle that every public claim is verifiable and the spec warns against padding a real list with soft items.

- **2026-09-28** — **`/about-us/credentials` built as a VIEW of the credential entity + admin home (LIVE).** Supersedes the static `Credentials Page Copy.md`, which typed the licence numbers into the prose — maintaining every number twice is the exact thing the entity exists to prevent. **No number, date, carrier or limit appears in static copy anywhere:** the view **header** carries the "Colorado issues no statewide landscape contractor licence" argument, the **footer** carries the insurance argument + HOA/property-manager block + CTAs, each record carries its own **`field_public_description`**, and every fact renders from a field. Filter `field_publish_publicly = 1`; sorted by new **`field_list_order`** (licences 10–30, insurance 40–60, fleet/manufacturer 70+ — alphabetical would put ABPA above the applicator business licence, which reads arbitrary); a **relationship to the credential type** supplies `field_verification_url`. Per spec, **no expiration dates** (an expired date on a live page is worse than none; the verification link pushes freshness to the issuing agency) and **no documents**. **Eight records seeded** with Todd's 9/28 **placeholder policy**: `0000000` in `field_credential_number` on the seven still awaiting a real number, and `field_publish_publicly` **FALSE** on all seven so zeros can never render as an apparent licence number on the one page a property manager might copy into a bid packet. Only the **ABPA** record publishes. The seeder never clobbers a number that has stopped being zeros and never flips an office `TRUE` back — **office rule: a record publishes when its number stops being zeros.** **Insurance split into three records** (GL / WC / Auto — two carriers, two policy numbers, three expiries), so the *argument* lives in the footer covering all of them and each policy record carries only short factual copy. **Admin home** at **`/admin/operations/system_content/credentials`** with **Company** and **Expiring soon** as tabs, wired `menu = admin` / `type = normal` to match every sibling view under that path (city/county/state/zipcodes, the spray-department lists); the earlier `/admin/office/credentials` was **moved**, not duplicated (now 404). **Field access, two changes — one a spec gap.** (1) The **itemless** check is now **neutral** rather than fail-closed: Views calls `fieldAccess()` with **no items** when deciding whether a field may appear in a display at all, so failing closed made the licence number unrenderable *anywhere*, including the page where it is public record. Safe because every path that exposes a VALUE re-checks **with** items — **proven, not assumed** (`verify_credential_number_access.php`, 7/7): with General Liability temporarily published carrying a fake policy number, the public page rendered its row **and coverage limits but NOT the number**, the ABPA number still rendered, and anon holds no credential entity-view permission so the JSON:API collection is closed. (2) **`field_credential_images` is now gated to office + crew** — §7 of the spec listed `field_credential_documents` but omitted images, and a photo of an insurance certificate shows the policy number the type-level `field_number_is_public` rule exists to suppress. Crew keep it (a tech pulling up their own applicator card is the field's purpose); the public never gets it. **Verified live anon:** `/about-us/credentials` **200**, 1 row (ABPA) **with** its real number, **0** placeholder/unpublished/expiry leakage, header + footer + record copy + title all correct; admin paths **403** to anon; and **the repointed chemicals-footer link now resolves 200** instead of 404. **Office still supplies** per the email list: number, expiration, renewal contact and a scan for each of the seven — then flip the publish flag. **Qualified Supervisor = Gerald Reeves** (uid 1443; Todd corrected the initial inference of uid 1 — flagged for exactly that reason). Reassigned on both envs via `reassign_credential_holder.php` (`BOS_CRED_CODE` + `BOS_CRED_UID`, dry-run default, refuses a user without the `teammates` role since the field's handler filters to it); the presave recomposed the title to "CDA Qualified Supervisor — Gerald Reeves". **That correction exposed a latent duplicate bug in the seeder:** it matched records by `(type, scope, teammate)`, so once the QS was anyone but uid 1 a re-run would have missed the record and created a **second** QS credential. Fixed to match on `(type, scope)` with the holder set **only on create** — the script owns the copy, the OFFICE owns the assignment, so a UI reassignment survives re-runs; where several people legitimately hold one type (a second ABPA tester) it matches the spec's holder and otherwise **skips with a warning rather than guessing**. Verified live: 8 records, exactly 1 CDA_QS, and the public page still shows only the ABPA row with **0** leakage of Gerald's name or the QS record (it is unpublished).

- **2026-09-27** — **Credentials link repointed to `/about-us/credentials` (LIVE).** Todd's call: `/about-us/credentials` is the wanted URL for the public credentials page. A targeted scan of live (config, node bodies + revisions, taxonomy text fields, block content, menu links) found **exactly one** public link — node **113** "Spray Chemicals", whose footer carries `<a href="/credentials">Our licences and certifications</a>`. Repointed via `fix_credentials_link_target.php` (idempotent — a **negative lookbehind** means an already-correct `/about-us/credentials` is never rewritten into `/about-us/about-us/credentials`; dry-run default; backup JSON; new revision with a log message). **Both source scripts updated too** so a re-run cannot regress it: `build_spray_parent_headers_footers.php` (the chemicals footer copy) and `build_spray_sitemap.php` (its custom-link list now targets `/about-us/credentials`). **Deliberately NOT touched:** `views.view.credentials` — that is the new credential entity's own admin view, whose paths (`admin/office/credentials`, `teammates/credentials`) merely contain the same substring; the scan flagged 3 such hits as false positives. Verified live: the rendered chemicals page has 1 × `href="/about-us/credentials"` and **0** stale `href="/credentials"`, anchor text intact. **⚠ Still a 404** — repointing fixes the destination, not the brokenness: `/about-us/credentials` does not exist yet. Building it needs marketing's `Credentials Page Copy.md` (§9 of the credential spec forbids Code writing public copy), and the `credential` public block (built, unplaced) can then be placed on it; note `field_publish_publicly` defaults **off**, so the block renders empty until office ticks it per record. **Corrected a stale claim** in the 2026-09-27 spray-views entry, which said the chemicals *and signal-words* footers both linked there — only chemicals does.

- **2026-09-27** — **Credential entity SHIPPED LIVE (licenses / certifications / insurance).** New `credential` ECK entity + `credential_types` vocabulary (10 seeded types) so a credential can be looked up from a phone, expiry is visible before it bites, and certification data stops living in two places. **Todd's decisions:** `field_teammate` targets **user** (not `teammate_profile` — no BOS precedent for referencing it, and it makes "my credentials" key off the current user); the §8 migration approved; **any teammate may see credentials**, own-only on their own profile page. **Scope is stated, never inferred** — `field_scope` required, validated in BOTH directions (presave backstop on every path + a form validate for a clean inline error), so company credentials are `scope = company` rather than `teammate IS NULL`. **Renewal supersedes, never overwrites** (`field_superseded_by`), and a filed backflow report keeps the number as it was on the test date — verified. **Field-level access, not view-mode hiding** (`hook_entity_field_access`, modelled on `material.module`) because **JSON:API is enabled and auto-exposes every content entity type**, so it is the only thing protecting a policy number: office sees all numbers; a teammate sees license/cert numbers but **never** insurance policy numbers or CDLs; documents, internal notes and renewal contact/URL are office-only regardless. That resolved a real contradiction between §7 (gate the number) and §5d (a tech pulls up their own applicator card) using the type-level `field_number_is_public` flag, so nobody can publish a policy number by ticking a box on one record. **`hook_cron`** moves status as dates pass (active → pending_renewal inside the type's lead window → expired), chosen so the expiring-soon view filters a STORED status instead of comparing a credential's expiry against its type's lead-days, which Views cannot express; the query is filtered and saves only on change (the 5.1M-item checkup-runaway lesson). **Five displays**: company list `/admin/office/credentials`, expiring-soon `/admin/office/credentials/expiring` (cards), `/teammates/credentials`, a profile block scoped to the **route** user, and an unplaced public block (neither `/credentials` nor `/about-us/credentials` exists, and §9 forbids writing public copy). **`bos_contact_attach` extended** — deleting a contact no longer leaves a dangling `field_renewal_contact`; that risk was confirmed real by inspection, not assumed. **No delete permission for any role** (the setup script actively revokes it): credentials are permanent history a filed report may reference. Todd's ABPA cert migrated: `06-2512234` / ABPA / **expires 2028-12-31** (supplied, never invented). Verified: dev 31/31 across four scripts; **live 18/18 read-only** (`verify_credential_live.php` creates nothing, so no test data reached production — live holds exactly one credential). Gate 0 inspection + the four spec corrections it produced are in `Architecture/credential_entity.md`. **⚠ New gotcha** (`drupal_bos_gotchas.md`): **a Views filter on a list_string field with operator `in` emits NO SQL** — `ManyToOne::operators()` defines only `or`/`and`/`not`, so `in` matches no method in `InOperator::query()`, no JOIN and no WHERE are added, and the display silently returns every row while the saved config and a handler dump both look correct. Also recorded: the handler plugin comes from views DATA (a `plugin_id` in config cannot swap it), contrib `token_views_filter` registers `id="list_field"` but only overrides `replaceTokens()` so it is NOT the cause, and `ViewExecutable::addHandler()` does not correct the operator either. **Remaining:** card CSS for the two card displays; the §8 profile-field retirement (deferred deliberately — the snapshot still falls back to those fields); office data entry (the insurance agent as a contact record — per Todd it is not in BOS, it lives in QuickBooks — plus the company GL/WC/Auto records and each type's verification URL). **Not built, per §9:** no notification engine, no document generation, no public expiration dates. **Tester-selector filtering deliberately skipped** — the spec assumes several certified testers and BOS has one, so a hard filter would block recording a test for anyone not yet entered; the non-blocking certification warning (mirroring the existing gauge-calibration warning) covers the actual failure mode.

Also now per-term `og_description` instead of the global generic line. **Two notes:** the authored titles deliberately **drop the "| Brookstone Outdoors" brand segment** the global taxonomy default adds — keyword-forward and needed to stay under 60 ch, so these 20 pages no longer carry the brand in `<title>`; and `entire-area` + `other-see-description` are scope terms, given honest metatags but not search targets. Marketing flagged `pasture`, `gravel` and `vacant-lot` as the three worth watching in Search Console. **Corrected a stale ROADMAP claim** while here: the SEO row said Metatag was "downloaded but entirely disabled" — it has been **installed and enabled since 2026-09-17** (`0f4f76e4`) and is what renders all of this.

- **2026-09-27** — **wind_direction: duplicate core `description` retired, meta description moved to a per-term override — SEO-neutral (DEV; live pending).** Todd asked whether wind_direction really was in the same state as spraying_locations; **it was not, and checking live corrected me.** Its core `description` was **populated** (142–162 ch on all 9 terms) with the **same one-liner as `field_short_description`** — verified **plain-text identical on all 9 on live** — so it had a meta description all along via `[term:description]`. The real defect was the **duplicate source**: two fields holding the same copy, so editing one leaves the other stale, and inconsistent with the single-source model just established for spraying_locations. **Fixed in the load-bearing order:** (1) `field_meta_tags` instance added; (2) each term's `description` override **entered from `field_short_description`**; (3) **only then** the stored core `description` cleared — reversed, step 3 would have stripped the meta description outright (taxonomy default = `[term:description]`, no global fallback); (4) `description` dropped from the `default` display (the only one still listing it; `full` already renders `field_short_description`, which on this vocab IS the public body — it has no `field_public_description`). **Proof it changed nothing publicly:** the rendered `<meta name="description">` is **byte-identical between dev-after and live-before** on east/north-east/none/west (156/162/142/156 ch); anon bodies still render their copy (incl. None's inversion warning) with **0 crew leak**. **Scripts generalized rather than copy-pasted** — the three spray-specific ones became **vocabulary-agnostic, `BOS_VID`-driven** (`setup_vocab_meta_tags.php`, `seed_vocab_meta_descriptions_from_short.php`, `drop_vocab_core_description.php`; renamed via `git mv`, behaviour unchanged for spraying_locations) plus a new **`retire_vocab_core_description.php`** that clears a stored core `description` **only where it is provably redundant** — empty, or plain-text identical to `field_short_description`/`field_public_description` — and **refuses** (reports, never deletes) any term carrying unique text, with a backup JSON. New read-only **`audit_vocab_text_fields.php`** (`BOS_VID`) reports per-term field lengths + the meta-description source + every display's field list, so a live environment's state is never assumed again. Only **4 of 9** descriptions run over 160 ch (162 — two characters, effectively fine).

Fixed **per-vocabulary, not sitewide** (repointing the taxonomy default would govern every vocab incl. ~1,300 operational terms): added the **`field_meta_tags`** instance (`setup_spraying_locations_meta_tags.php` — storage already existed on `taxonomy_term` via services/backflow_uses/geo; adds only the instance + a `metatag_firehose` sidebar widget on the term form, and is on **no view display**, mirroring `setup_backflow_uses_content_fields.php`), then **entered each term's `description` override from its own `field_short_description`** (`seed_spraying_locations_meta_descriptions.php`) — plain-texted (tags stripped, entities decoded, whitespace collapsed) and otherwise **verbatim: not truncated, not rewritten**. Only `description` is set; `title` keeps the global `{name} | Brookstone Outdoors | Delta & Montrose CO` pattern and `og_description` the global line. **Non-destructive + idempotent:** a term that already has a non-empty description override is preserved (an office edit on the form is never clobbered) unless `BOS_META_FORCE=1`; dry-run by default. Verified dev: all 20 render a per-term `<meta name="description">`, the field is editable on the term form (sidebar), the landing page keeps its own description from `page_1`'s metatag extender, and the 20 landing cards are unaffected. **⚠ Flagged, not fixed:** **11 of 20 run 162–234 chars** so Google will clip the tail — worst are **Arena 234** and **Driveway 190** (both derived from their first paragraph on migration); tightening those is a copy task for marketing, deliberately not auto-trimmed mid-thought. _(Correction, same session: I had claimed `wind_direction` "has the same hole" — **it did not**. Its actual problem was the duplicate source; see the wind_direction entry above.)_

Word-paste artifacts normalised (inline `style="margin-…pt"` on `<p>`, trailing `<br>&nbsp;`) so the two bodies match the other 18. `field_teammate_description` was already correct on both (5.4k/5.1k chars) and was **not touched**. **(2) Displays** — `description` dropped from **`full`**, **`admin_view`** *and* the non-tier **`default`** display; `full` = name + short + public + applicable services (no crew field), `teammate_view` unchanged, `admin_view` = everything-minus-description. **(3) Both builder scripts updated** (`setup_spraying_locations_fields.php`, `build_spray_audience_displays.php`) so a re-run can't reintroduce `description` — previously both listed it. Preferred fix is a **`field_meta_tags` instance on the vocabulary** (scoped, precedent: `backflow_uses`, geo via `setup_geo_metatag_field.php`), not repointing the sitewide taxonomy default at `[term:field_short_description]` (verified to resolve, but governs every vocab incl. ~1,300 operational terms). - **2026-09-27** — **Alias-based breadcrumbs for public taxonomy term pages — `bos_breadcrumbs` (LIVE).** Public term pages showed only **"Home"**: core's `taxonomy_term.breadcrumb` builder (priority 1002) builds from the flat vocabulary hierarchy, ignoring the URL alias. New code-only module (a tagged breadcrumb-builder service, **no config**) registers `BosTermAliasBreadcrumbBuilder` at **priority 1010** (above core) that rebuilds the trail from the page's **URL alias** — walk parents, resolve each to a route, access-check, use the route's real title (view title / term name), **Home** first, current page omitted, unresolvable/titleless segments **skipped** (never humanised). Caching: `url.path.parent` + `url.path.is_front` + `user.permissions` + access cacheability + per-term tags. **No BreadcrumbList JSON-LD** (that's the separate Metatag/`schema_metatag` ROADMAP pass — this just gives it a true hierarchy to serialise). Deploy: rsync + `drush en bos_breadcrumbs` + `cr` (code, not `cim`). Verified live anon: `/material/backflow`→Home›Materials, `/material/plants/annuals`→Home›Materials›Plants, `/services/backflow-prevention/uses/irrigation-lawn-sprinkler`→4-level, view pages + homepage + `/winterize`/`/about`/`/contact` + operational terms unchanged; parent-term cache tag present. **Finding:** the spec's hypothetical dead-segment case (Annuals' `/material/plants` not resolving) doesn't occur — all 12 nested `material_types` aliases have resolving parents (0 dead segments), so no pathauto issue to raise.
