- **2026-09-26** — **Material entity — audience view-mode tiers across all 22 bundles (LIVE, `material` module).** Wired the BOS audience pattern onto the `material` ECK entity: one canonical `/material/{bundle}/{name}` URL, body swapped by role — office (`supervisor`/`administration`/`site_assistant`/`site_admin`/`administrator`) → **admin**, crew (`teammates`) → **teammate**, `client` → **client**, everyone else → **full** ("Public") — with a mandatory `user.roles` cache context on the render so the cache can't leak one audience's body to another, and the shared `.bos-admin-view` wrapper + `brookstone_olivero/audience_admin` library on the admin render. Added to the **existing `material` module** (checked first, per request — it already governs the entity) as `material_entity_view_mode_alter` + `material_material_view_alter` + `material_preprocess_eck_entity` (ECK theme hook `eck_entity`; view modes `admin`/`client`/`teammate` were already registered, "Public" = core `full`). Because view modes are entity-level, the routing covers **all 22 bundles** at once; the 18 bundles without a configured admin/teammate/client display fall back to their `default` display (core behaviour) and light up as each is built (decorative_rock, mulch, plants, trees already have all four). **Access is untouched** — material view perms are per-bundle and deliberately set elsewhere; this only picks the view mode for whoever is already allowed to view (most bundles aren't anon-viewable, so the Public tier currently serves logged-in non-privileged users + anon on the few anon-open bundles). Code-only (one `.module`); deploy: rsync + `cr` (no cim/DB/new module). Verified live via account-switching: client-only→client, teammate→teammate, office→admin, anon→full; admin render carries the wrapper + cache context, anon does not (no leak). Commit `a8121276`.

- **2026-09-26** — **Removed the RSS feed link/icon from all taxonomy term pages (LIVE).** Every taxonomy term page (services, backflow, tags, …) carried a `<link rel="alternate" type="application/rss+xml" href="/taxonomy/term/N/feed">` + a visible feed icon, injected by the core `taxonomy_term` view's `feed_1` display (attached to `page_1`). SEO-neutral (Google ignores `rel=alternate` RSS for ranking), but unused clutter on the public service pages and a thin crawlable `/feed` endpoint. **Disabled the `feed_1` display** (`display_options.enabled = FALSE`) via idempotent entity-API script `disable_taxonomy_term_feed.php` — removes the `<link>`, the feed icon, and the `/taxonomy/term/%/feed` route site-wide. Fully reversible (re-enable the display). Deploy: rsync script → run → `cr` (no cim/DB). Verified live: backflow + services term pages have no RSS link/icon, `/feed` 404s, pages 200. Commit `7a36fa1e`.

- **2026-09-26** — **Backflow *uses* landing view styled like the device-types page (LIVE).** The uses landing (`/services/backflow-prevention/uses`) was rendering as a bulleted `<ul>` (Views **`html_list`** style). Switched it to **Unformatted list** and reused the device-types landing's exact classes — wrapper `css_class` `backflow-types-landing`, `row_class` `backflow-device-type-item`, and the name field wrapped in a `<div class="backflow-device-type-title">` (`element_type: div`, `element_class`) — so the existing `bos_backflow_types/css/landing.css` styles it identically (orange Oswald titles, no underline, teaser below, no bullets) with **no new CSS**. Attached that stylesheet on the uses landing route by adding `BOS_BACKFLOW_USES_LANDING_ROUTE` to `bos_backflow_types_preprocess_page`. Extended `build_backflow_uses_landing.php` (idempotent); rsync module + script → run → `cr`. Verified live: unformatted `views-row` items, `.backflow-device-type-title` divs, CSS attached, list order preserved. Commit `af624802`.

- **2026-09-26** — **Backflow *uses* — full marketing copy loaded across all 14 terms + landing wired (LIVE).** Loaded the authoritative "Backflow Uses — 14 Terms ALL FIELDS" copy (rebuilt 2026-09-26) into every `backflow_uses` term: `field_list_order`, `field_short_description` (landing-card teaser), `field_public_description` (term-page body), `field_teammate_description` (crew, `teammate_view` only), and `field_meta_tags` (per-page title / meta description / og:description — no two descriptions repeat, all titles ≤60 chars). The two body fields are stored **`full_html`** (the copy carries `<h3>`/`<ul>`/`<a class="button">` that `basic_html` would strip). `field_short_description`/`field_list_order`/`field_meta_tags` instances added to the bundle (all three storages already existed → instances only, added to the term edit form; `field_meta_tags` = metatag firehose, consumed automatically by Metatag — no hook). Wired the **landing view** `land_backflow_uses` (`/services/backflow-prevention/uses`) to show the short-description teaser (was the core description field, empty on every term) and sort by `field_list_order` then name — Irrigation(10)→…→Mobile(140). admin_view now also shows short-desc + list-order. Terms matched by the stable `field_use_code` (env-independent). Idempotent entity-API scripts: `setup_backflow_uses_content_fields.php`, `seed_backflow_uses_content.php`, `build_backflow_uses_landing.php`, + the extended `setup_backflow_uses_view_modes.php`. Deploy: rsync scripts → run in order → `cr` (no cim/DB). Verified DDEV + live: per-term SEO title/description/og render from the field (overriding the global pattern), public body renders with the button, **no teammate-copy leak** to anon, landing in list order with teasers. Commit `98b7d169`. **Note:** the landing view's header/footer/title *chrome* is the separate "Backflow Uses - Landing View Copy" deliverable — not touched here.

- **2026-09-26** — **Backflow *uses* vocab gets the audience view-mode tiers (LIVE, `bos_backflow_types`).** Applied the same one-URL/role-swapped-body pattern to the **`backflow_uses`** vocabulary (14 terms under `/services/backflow-prevention/uses/{use}`): office/admin → **admin_view** (public desc + teammate desc + `field_use_code`), crew → **teammate_view** (`field_teammate_description`), public → **full** (`field_public_description`), with the mandatory `user.roles` cache context so the page cache can't leak one audience's body to another. **Generalized `bos_backflow_types`** to govern both backflow vocabs via a new `BOS_BACKFLOW_TYPES_VIDS` array (view-mode routing + admin wrapper + cache context now check the set); the **hero banner + landing-view CSS stay device-types-only** (singular `BOS_BACKFLOW_TYPES_VID`) — `backflow_uses` has no banner field, so those hooks no-op for it (plain `bos_services`-style 3-tier, no hero). Three view displays built by idempotent entity-API script `setup_backflow_uses_view_modes.php` (the bundle previously had none — it fell back to the default display, ignoring role). Deploy: rsync module + script → run script → `cr` (no cim/DB). Verified DDEV + live: routing office/crew/public correct, anon term page 200 renders the public description with **no teammate-copy leak**. Commit `226b4ed8`.

- **2026-09-26** — **Backflow device types — audience view-mode tiers + rotating hero banner (LIVE, `bos_backflow_types`).** New module (mirrors `bos_services`) wires the `backflow_device_types` term page to the BOS audience pattern: one `/services/backflow-prevention/{type}` URL, body by role — office/admin → **admin_view** (all fields), crew → **teammate_view** (`field_teammate_description`), public → **full** (`field_public_description`) — with `user.roles` cache context (mandatory) so the page cache can't leak one audience's body to another. `field_banner_images` renders for public viewers as a **full-bleed rotating hero** in the theme's `hero` region (`bo_hero_banner`, `max_2600x2600`, `field_short_description` as subtitle); page-title block hidden for public when a banner shows (hero is the H1), banner hidden from the public body. Displays built by idempotent `setup_backflow_type_view_modes.php` (entity-API, so the view-mode displays register — a raw-config display save leaves them unregistered); `field_meta_tags` stays hidden on displays (SEO via the form). Also gave the term canonical page its **public description** (the pre-module default display rendered none). Deploy: rsync module+script → `drush en bos_backflow_types` → display script → `cr` (no cim/DB). Verified DDEV 9/9 + live 7/7: routing office/crew/public, each display's fields, public hero renders with a banner (reversibly tested + cleaned up), anon term page 200 with the public desc, landing 200. Commit `d0325018`. Docs: ADR §2.5.

- **2026-09-26** — **Backflow device types get the content tier — banner/meta-tags/short-desc/teammate-desc fields (LIVE).** Added `field_banner_images` (image, multi), `field_meta_tags` (metatag SEO override — feeds the term's meta tags automatically, no hook, same as services), `field_short_description` (text_long teaser), and `field_teammate_description` (text_long crew copy) to the `backflow_device_types` vocab, giving it the same content tier as the services vocab. **All four storages already existed on `taxonomy_term` — reused, not created** (banner_images on equipment_types, meta_tags on services, short_description on brookstone_tags/growth_zone, teammate_description on backflow_uses + many); only the instances + form widgets were added. Banner uses the standard `image_image` upload widget (not equipment_types' `entity_browser_file`, whose browser config is null); meta uses `metatag_firehose` (sidebar). Idempotent entity-API script (`setup_backflow_device_type_content_fields.php`; field configs silently skip on cim — sync instance YAMLs committed for record). Deploy: rsync script → run on live → `cr` (additive; no data/module change). Verified: all four render on term add + edit forms (DDEV + live), re-run idempotent, landing + term pages 200 (no regression). **Note:** the fields are editable now; rendering the banner/short/teammate copy on the term/landing pages is a separate display decision (the metatag field works automatically). Commit `2cc8c275`. Docs: ADR §2.5.

- **2026-09-26** — **Backflow Hose Bibb Vacuum Breaker (HBVB) device type — 7th term, non-testable (LIVE).** Closes the type-axis gap opposite the existing `HOSE_BIBB` **use** term: a tech inventorying a hose bibb connection now has a correct `field_device_type` (previously only AVB — mechanically wrong — or blank). Depended on `field_is_testable` (shipped same day). **HBVB is non-testable** (`field_is_testable`=FALSE) — and because the field_is_testable hooks are **generic** (compute non-testable TIDs from the flag), HBVB is automatically hidden from the device form's test-scheduling fields and excluded from the compliance dashboard **with no code change**. Added `HBVB` to `field_type_code` `allowed_values` (`add_hbvb_type_code.php`, idempotent; sync YAML updated); the **seed script (now 7 terms — the mechanism of record)** creates the term with name, code, flag, and the **verbatim marketing description** (`full_html`; CTA rendered without a `class="button"` since the six sibling terms use no CTA class — matched them, no new class). Description set on create/when-empty only (never clobbers a marketing edit). Metatag title/desc skipped — the vocab has no `field_meta_tags` override (global taxonomy metatag default still applies the brand suffix). Deploy: rsync 2 scripts → allowed_values → seed → `cr` (additive; no module change, no DB migration). Verified: DDEV 19/19 + live 9/9 — code/flag valid, alias `/services/backflow-prevention/hose-bibb-vacuum-breaker-hbvb` resolves, term add/edit + device forms render, HBVB selectable, dashboard excludes it, landing shows **7** cards with the HBVB teaser, description stored intact (2 h3 + all links, un-stripped), seed idempotent. **Note on display:** the landing cards show the description **trimmed to 300 chars** (`text_default`, a pre-existing view setting identical for all 7 terms), and term canonical pages render a meta description, not the full h3 body — so the stored h3/links are preserved but not shown in full on those surfaces; rendering the full body is a separate display decision, out of scope here. **Open item (recorded, not decided):** whether HBVBs get inventoried everywhere vs. commercial/high-hazard only — Todd's call (ADR §6). Commit `d796699f`. Docs: ADR §2.5/§3.11/§6.

- **2026-09-26** — **Backflow `field_is_testable` — guard non-testable devices from testing/reminders (LIVE).** `backflow_device_types` had **six** terms in production (the four testable assemblies PVB/RP/DCVA/SVB **plus** AVB + Dual Check, added outside the seed after 2026-06-20) but nothing distinguished a testable assembly from a non-testable device — so an AVB/dual-check `property_backflow_device` took a `field_next_due_date` + 12-month frequency like an RP and the compliance dashboard listed it as due (the reminders engine, unbuilt, would have chased an impossible test). **Fix:** new boolean **`field_is_testable`** on the vocabulary (default **TRUE** = fail safe), TRUE for PVB/RP/DCVA/SVB, **FALSE for AVB + DuC**. Consumers: the device form hides `field_next_due_date` + `field_test_frequency_months` for a non-testable type (`backflow_device_form_alter`, server-side `#access`, not conditional_fields); the compliance dashboard excludes non-testable devices (`backflow_device_views_query_alter` — a NOT IN subquery with non-testable TIDs resolved from the flag at runtime, so it's env-independent and avoids the entity's documented Views-relationship fragility). **Investigation corrected the stale ADR:** `field_type_code` needed no reconciliation (allowed_values already covers all six codes; no empty required fields), and **zero** live devices are of a non-testable type (only PVB=6 + RP=17), so no production data to clean up. Field created via idempotent entity-API script (`setup_backflow_is_testable_field.php`; field configs silently skip on cim — sync YAMLs committed for record); the **seed script extended to all six terms + the flag** and is now the mechanism of record (fixes the "terms added outside the seed" drift). Deploy: rsync module+scripts → field script → `cr` → seed on live. Verified live (13/13): term add/edit + device add/edit/full forms render, all 6 flags correct, dashboard executes (23 rows) with no non-testable leak, landing view + term aliases 200 anon, seed idempotent. Commit `05f034ac`. **Follow-up (out of scope, worth it):** with the flag in place, `material.field_backflow_type` could now map the 710 AVB and 810 Dual Check materials that were deliberately left null. Docs: ADR §2.5/§5/§6.

- **2026-09-26** — **Backflow device clean URLs + per-property device list page (LIVE).** For the laminated QR service tags: device aliases went from `…/25-stafford-ln/backflow/bf-000027-825y-reduced-pressure-assembly-rpa` to `…/25-stafford-ln/**backflow**/bf-000027` — keep the `/backflow/` segment, drop the description, last segment = padded BF number. **(1)** New `[property_backflow_device:bf_number]` token (`backflow_device.module`, reuses `_backflow_device_code()`); pathauto renders it `bf-000027`. **(2)** Pathauto pattern `backflow_device_paths` → `…/backflow/[property_backflow_device:bf_number]`; all 23 device aliases regenerated, old aliases 301 automatically (redirect module already on + `update_action=2`). **QR-safe:** device QRs encode the immutable canonical URL, so existing printed tags are unaffected. **(3)** New **per-property device list page** at the pretty `/{property}/backflow` URL (so a property with multiple backflows lists them all, and stripping the BF number off a tag lands on the list): added a public `page_1` display (`properties/%/backflow`) to the `backflow_property_devices_eva` view, gated on `view any property_backflow_device entities` (the same perm that makes device pages public). **(4)** `BackflowListPathProcessor` (inbound+outbound, in `backflow_device`) maps `/{property-alias}/backflow` ↔ `/properties/{id}/backflow` — resolved from the property's current alias at request time, so no per-property list alias to store/maintain and it survives property-alias changes; the outbound half stops the redirect route-normalizer 301-ing the pretty URL to the raw system path. Scripts: `set_backflow_pathauto_pattern.php`, `build_backflow_list_page.php`, `regenerate_backflow_aliases.php` (idempotent, per-env). Deploy: live DB dump (`~/pre_backflow_alias_20260926.sql.gz`) → rsync module+scripts → `cr` → pattern + list-page scripts → regen 23 aliases → verify. Verified live: device 27 new URL 200, old URL 301→new, list page 200 lists the device, title unchanged. Commits `1a4927d4`, `3b5587c0`. **Gotcha:** adding a Views page display via raw configFactory leaves the route unregistered (404) — save the View as an **entity** so `postSave` rebuilds routes (see `Governance/drupal_bos_gotchas.md`).

- **2026-09-26** — **Property/admin page speed — two root causes fixed (LIVE).** Property pages took **30–120s** (cold-cache loads 500'd at the timeout). Diagnosed with a signal-based sampling profiler (`pcntl_signal(SIGALRM)` + `debug_backtrace`, since the box has no xhprof) after DB logging showed SQL was only ~0.2s of a ~5s render. **(1) Image EXIF effect (property-specific):** the 2026-09-22 `auto_rotate_lite` effect, added to all 13 image styles, reads each image's EXIF from the **source file on every render** (`transformDimensions`/`getImageOrientation`) — on **S3** that's a remote read per image per load, and it scales with photo count (→ work-order count). Busiest property (620 WOs) entity render was **33.3s**. Removed the effect from all styles via configFactory raw config (`web/scripts/remove_auto_orient_from_image_styles.php`; NOT the ImageStyle entity API — that triggers a full derivative flush); derivatives intentionally not flushed (already-rotated ones stay correct; new uploads corrected at source by `exif_orientation`). Render **33.3s → 0.36s**. **(2) admin_toolbar_tools (site-wide, all admin pages):** it expands the whole admin structure into the toolbar — on BOS **545 per-view edit links, per-bundle field-UI/form-display/view-display links, and one permission form per ECK entity type**, and with **5,710 permissions** (ECK per-bundle perms across 90+ types) each permission-form access check groups all 5,710 — all access-checked inline every render. New **`bos_toolbar_perf`** module (`hook_menu_links_discovered_alter`) prunes those link families (guarded to `admin_toolbar_tools.`-generated links only, so no core/BOS menu item is touched; office keeps the normal admin + BOS menus and the add-content/collection/flush-cache shortcuts). `/admin/content` **5.8s → 1.0s**. **Combined result (real authenticated web):** the 620-WO property that was **121s→500** now loads in **~1.4s** warm; light property 6.1s → 1.4s. Deploy: rsync + `remove_auto_orient` script + `drush en bos_toolbar_perf` + `cr` (no cim/DB). Commits `99a6999d`, `21e0ac51`. Gotchas: `Governance/drupal_bos_gotchas.md` (auto_rotate_lite EXIF-on-S3; admin_toolbar_tools scale). **Follow-up (deferred):** the 5,710-permission count itself (ECK per-bundle permission generation) is the underlying disease worth revisiting; and `exif_orientation` handles new uploads but does not retroactively rotate legacy source files.

- **2026-09-23** — **Old-domain link cleanup + credential removal + fertilizing sq-ft write-back + apparel report polish (LIVE).** (1) **`sewardslandscape.com` links removed** — scanned all 6,359 DB text columns; rewrote **40 content rows** (public Sprinkler Design service desc, a node image, 4 manufacturer descs, a property desc, 24 work_order "Work To Be Done" fields, 4 status-update notes + revisions) from `https://sewardslandscape.com/…` to **relative `/…`** so nothing links the now-un-owned domain (`web/scripts/rewrite_seward_links.php`, backed up, direct SQL — no entity re-saves). Verified 0 `//sewardslandscape.com` anywhere. Remaining hits are text mentions + email-field addresses (not links). Scan tools: `scan_domain_refs.php`, `scan_domain_snippets.php`. (2) **Plaintext credential removed** from a snow-removal instructions field (`remove_snow_instruction_credentials.php`, backed up); two other real crew credentials left in place pending a decision (deleting removes the only record). (3) **`wo_fertilizing` turf-sqft write-back** — when a fertilizing WO is completed with a tech-entered `field_current_turf_sq_footage`, it now writes back to `property_landscape_details.current.field_turf_sq_footage` (only when changed; creates the record if missing), so the property page + future WOs stay current. Billing already used the WO's value in presave. (4) **Team Apparel report** — teammate names now link to their profile edit form (destination back to the report); each tally has an explicit **Total to order** row. Commits `f3ea4d16`, `2ce433c0`, `452f10de`, `d1d806e6`.

- **2026-09-22** — **Portrait-photo auto-orientation + Team Apparel report + service-card content/HOA parity (LIVE).** (1) **EXIF orientation fix** — portrait phone photos rendered sideways in image-style derivatives (GD drops the EXIF flag when generating a derivative). Installed **`drupal/exif_orientation`** (rotates the source at upload → new photos correct everywhere) + **`drupal/auto_rotate_lite`** (image effect added first, weight −50, on all **13** image styles via `web/scripts/add_auto_orient_to_image_styles.php` → derivatives of existing photos rotate per EXIF). Flushed all derivatives on live via detached `nohup drush image:flush --all` (per-file S3 deletes = very slow, ~thousands; post-completion hang killed once the log confirmed all 13 flushed). Two small composer-managed contrib image utilities. **Gotcha** (`drupal_bos_gotchas.md`): saving an image style entity triggers a full flush in `postSave` → add the effect via `configFactory` to avoid it, and always flush **detached** on S3. (2) **Team Apparel report** — new `bos_team_apparel` module: Admin toolbar → **People → Shirt & Hat Sizes** (`/admin/people/apparel`), active teammates' shirt size + hat preference with **per-size order totals** for ordering; custom controller (not Views — the tally); perm `view team apparel report` granted to office roles on install. (3) **Service cards** (`bos_services` `service_children` EVA) — 55 card **summaries** written to `field_service_public_desc` summary (Western-Slope voice; `apply_service_summaries.php`) + 18 draft full **descriptions** for stub/empty services (`apply_service_bodies.php`); blurb = the field **summary** (plain text, single-escaped — fixed a double-escape); full-height left iconic-image strip (68px, dedicated `service_card_strip` image style) via `:has()` with full-width text fallback; single column on phones. (4) **HOA page parity** — added `hoa` to all property-detail EVAs (`add_hoa_to_property_evas.php`) + cloned `properties.property.default` view-display layout (17 field groups) onto `properties.hoa.default` (`clone_property_display_to_hoa.php`) so HOA pages render like residential; `hoa.public` showcase untouched. (5) Nav polish — main menu depth kept at 2 (Olivero nav is two-level); mobile off-canvas secondary row stacked; Services toggle icon deduped. Commits `df2504fa`, `a2245b20`, `77388e8e`, `12a60be4`.

- **2026-09-21** — **Services pages: "Our {Category} Services" child-service cards + main menu kept two-level (LIVE).** The public service term pages now surface their **sub-services as a compact card grid** below the description — one card (name + short blurb) per **direct child** of the current term, auto-populating and nesting at every level (Landscape & Lawn Care → Mowing/Spraying/…; Sprinkler Systems → Check Up/Repair/Winterizing; Spraying → its own children; a leaf service shows nothing). Built as an **EVA** (`service_children` view) attached to `taxonomy_term:services` — base `taxonomy_term_field_data`, contextual arg = `taxonomy_term__parent.parent_target_id` fed the current term id (so it returns that term's children), fields name(linked) + `field_service_public_desc` (summary/trimmed 140), header hidden when empty. `bos_services_views_pre_render()` attaches the compact-grid CSS (`bos_services/service_children`, 2-up mobile / 3–4-up desktop) and rewrites the header to **"Our {current term} Services"**. **Why this and not a deeper nav:** Olivero's primary nav is a **two-level** design (JS collapses only the second level; a third level renders flat), so `depth: 3` on the main menu block dumped every third-level item open = a huge list — reverted to **`depth: 2`**. In-page contextual cards are also better SEO than a giant nav (descriptive anchors, topical clustering, no link-equity dilution) and every service page is already published + in the sitemap + `noindex`-free. Built via idempotent entity-API script `web/scripts/build_service_children_view.php` (per env, no cim). Deploy: rsync `bos_services` + script, run script, restart `lsphp`, `cr`. **Gotcha (recurrence):** a **tag-cached view** re-poisons its render cache if a stale `lsphp` worker renders it between `drush cr` and the first good render — the reliable prime is `cr` then an immediate **CLI** `$view->render()` (fresh code, no web hit between) to bake the correct `#attached`/header into the cache. Commits `db33a659` (cards) + `dc6a34a0` (depth-2 revert). Docs: `Governance/ui_patterns.md`.

- **2026-09-20** — **Geo pages: editable Meta tags field + footer name-links + `bos_state`→`bos_geo` consolidation (LIVE).** (1) **Editable SEO override** — added a per-entity `field_meta_tags` (type `metatag`, `metatag_firehose` widget) box to the **state / county / city** ECK entities' edit forms (same as the services taxonomy), via idempotent entity-API script `web/scripts/setup_geo_metatag_field.php` (no cim). The `hook_metatags_alter` now **defers to the office's override** — only auto-fills the `description`/`og_description`/`og_image` tags left blank (auto = description field + banner via `max_1300x1300`). (2) **Footer "Service Area" links** — the footer block's county/city names now link to their geo landing pages (`bos_geo` `hook_preprocess_block` on `block_content:footer_service_area`): counties line → county pages + "Colorado" → the state, city line → city pages; matches on the short name (strips "County"/"City of"/"Town of"), **published records only**, wording left exactly as typed, cache-tagged `county_list`/`city_list`/`state_list` so it rebuilds as records publish. (3) **Consolidated `bos_state` into `bos_geo`** — merged the hero, SEO metatags, `page_title_block` suppression, and county-card CSS (library moved to `bos_geo/county_cards`) into `bos_geo`; dropped the duplicated view-mode/view hooks; uninstalled + deleted `bos_state`. **Supervisors keep the admin view** on geo pages (preserves prior behavior — `bos_state` used to win the view-mode alter with supervisor in the office tier). Geo presentation now lives in one module. Deploy: rsync `bos_geo` + footer template, `drush pmu bos_state`, restart `lsphp` workers, `cr`, prime. Verified live: meta field on all three forms, footer links resolve, hero + single title + styled cards on state/county pages. **Gotcha surfaced (see `drupal_bos_gotchas.md`):** a module-ownership swap on live needs the web `lsphp` workers restarted *before* priming caches — a stale worker otherwise bakes wrong results (missing CSS, wrong block-access) into the render/block cache, and those survive `drush cr`. Commits `d892e84e` (field) + `73346c57` (footer + consolidation). Docs: `Governance/ui_patterns.md`.

- **2026-09-17** — **Security: Drupal core 10.6.16 → 10.6.17 (SA-CORE-2026-013) + Metatag SEO titles (LIVE).** A `composer require` for Metatag was blocked by composer's advisory gate, surfacing **SA-CORE-2026-013** (moderately critical, third-party libraries, reported 2026-09-16, affects core `<10.6.17`). Updated core **10.6.16 → 10.6.17** (+ Symfony 6.4.46 / laminas-feed 2.26.2 patch bumps); `composer audit` clean afterward. Installed **`drupal/metatag` ^2 (2.2.0)** as the SEO foundation and set page-title defaults to keep the brand + add a local-SEO geo suffix — **`[title] | Brookstone Outdoors | Delta & Montrose CO`** — across the **global, node and taxonomy_term** metatag defaults (per-entity-type defaults override global, so all three set; `user` left brand-only). Title config via idempotent entity-API script (`web/scripts/set_metatag_title_defaults.php`, no cim). Deploy (standard security process): live DB dump → rsync composer.json/lock → maintenance ON → `composer install --no-dev` → `updb` → `drush en metatag` → title script → `cr` → maintenance OFF. Verified live: 10.6.17, no advisories, service/home/spraying titles render the geo suffix, site 200. Commit `0f4f76e4`. **Follow-ups:** the homepage title is generic ("Home | …") and money pages exceed Google's ~60-char display limit — hand-tune key pages via Metatag per-entity overrides; meta descriptions + Open Graph tags are now available via Metatag as the next SEO step.

- **2026-09-16** — **Optional "Add Freight" (at cost) on WO material line items (LIVE).** Techs can recover shipping on special-order parts **without marking it up and without eating it**, riding inside the part's own line (ONE clean line to the customer). Two new fields on `wo_material_list_item`/`items`: **`field_add_freight`** (boolean, default OFF) + **`field_freight`** (decimal 10,2, min 0; help: "Total freight for this shipment — enter ONCE per order, not on every line. Added to the customer price at cost (no markup)"), created via idempotent entity-API script `web/scripts/setup_material_item_freight_fields.php` (no cim; added to all 4 item form displays + default view display). Subtotal math updated **identically in both paths** (`wo_material_item_subtotal`, `wo_material_list_management`) via a `_wo_material_*_freight()` helper: freight is added **after** the markup multiply and **never** marked up — `field_subtotal_w_markup = (qty×cost×markup) + freight` (customer price), `field_subtotal = (qty×cost) + freight` (landed cost, so `wo_profit` COGS/margin stays honest — freight = $0 margin). Customer invoice (`get_total_*_material_list_price` + `wo_profit` revenue) sums `field_subtotal_w_markup`, so freight flows to `field_wo_total`. Form UX: `field_freight` hidden until the box is checked (`wo_material_list_form_form_alter` #states); an unchecked line is byte-identical to before. Display: `MaterialListManagementForm` appends "(incl. $X freight)" to the part label when on. **Note:** only `wo_material_list_management` is enabled on dev+live (item_subtotal path is dormant but kept in sync). Verified live: $127.12 part ×1.30 + $15.08 freight → landed $142.20, customer $180.34, margin $38.14; box off = 127.12/165.256 unchanged. Policy in `Modules/wo_bundle_modules.md`. Standing policy: **recover shipping at cost on special-order parts via Add Freight — enter freight once per shipment; never mark up freight; never eat it.**

- **2026-09-08** — **Analytics privacy fix: GTM gated to anonymous only (no GA4/PII behind login) + inert `google_analytics` removed (LIVE).** `bos_analytics` injected the GTM container (GTM-WC8F8PCN → GA4 + Meta Pixel + Google Ads conversion) on **every** page with no role gate, so authenticated BOS/admin pages — customer names, addresses, job numbers, emails in paths + titles — were feeding **GA4** (a Google data-policy violation; remedy is property termination, not a warning). Fix, per the locked design (role gate, **not** a path list): both injection hooks — `bos_analytics_page_attachments()` (head loader) + `bos_analytics_page_top()` (`<noscript>`) — now render **only when `\Drupal::currentUser()->isAnonymous()`**, with a **`user.roles:anonymous`** cache context added unconditionally so it **fails closed** (the tag is not rendered for any authenticated user, not merely filtered downstream). Anonymous prospects on public marketing pages are unaffected; the `winterize_submit` conversion push still fires (form submitters are anon). No admin route shares the public form IDs, so the awct trigger can't fire behind login. Role gating survives every future BOS route with no maintenance; the admin-route backstop was deliberately **omitted** as redundant (anon can't reach admin routes) — avoids an extra cache context. **Also:** the contrib **`google_analytics` (4.0.3)** module was enabled but with an **empty account** (injecting nothing) — uninstalled (`drush pmu`) + **composer-removed** on both envs, and its stale refs dropped from `core.extension` + the three user form displays. **Not the injector** (GTM is our custom `bos_analytics`; `drupal/google_tag` was never installed). Verified live: anon homepage = 1 `gtm.js` loader; authenticated (teammate/client) = **none** + `user.roles:anonymous` context; site 200. Deploy: rsync `bos_analytics.module` + `drush pmu google_analytics` + composer install (both envs) + `cr` (no cim). GTM container ID stays in `settings.php` (`bos_gtm_container_id`), so dev remains untracked. Commit `0c5bb087`.

- **2026-09-07** — **HOA Phase 3 — public showcase on the CANONICAL page, one URL per record (LIVE, `bos_hoa`).** HOAs behave like every other property: **one canonical page per record** (Stonebridge = 7 pages), **role-aware** (the Services pattern) — NOT a separate route. _(Superseded an initial `/hoa/{slug}` controller build the same day; retired per direction to match how all properties work.)_ **How:** `hook_entity_view_mode_alter` switches the canonical `full` render to a **`public`** view mode for non-internal roles (staff keep the full page); `hook_properties_view` adds a `user.roles` cache context + embeds the public photo gallery (`property_photos_public`) in the public mode; `hook_ENTITY_TYPE_access` makes a **published hoa** anon-viewable (residential unaffected → member homes' canonical pages stay **403** to anon). The **`public` view mode display** (`properties.hoa.public`) shows **safe showcase fields ONLY** (neighborhood, public description, service timeframe) — every other field removed so the bundle's inherited internal fields can't leak. `hook_block_access` denies **property-internal blocks** (WO-links, info/map headers, contract, wo_property_info) to non-internal users, so nothing internal renders on the public HOA page (staff keep them). Verified live: Bear Creek HOA canonical = **anon 200, name shown, ZERO internal leak** (no gate code / contacts / WO blocks); residential member home canonical = **anon 403**. Setup: `setup_hoa_public_view_mode.php`. Commit `1d3c0c8e`. **Office to finish the showcase:** fill each HOA's public description + stage public photos (`field_public_ok` media). **HOA epic (Phases 1–3) complete.** Docs: ROADMAP, CLAUDE.md properties section.

- **2026-09-07** — **HOA Phase 2 — GPS point-in-polygon membership engine (LIVE, `bos_hoa`).** A residential home (`properties.property`) whose `field_geofield` point falls inside a **discounted HOA's** `field_boundary` polygon is auto-linked (new **`field_hoa`** ref on the property bundle) and flagged **`field_hoa_contracted=TRUE`** → gets the winterizing HOA discount ($60). Homes the scan linked that no longer qualify (outside the boundary, or the HOA un-discounted) are released; **manual flags (no `field_hoa`) are never touched**. `HoaMembershipService`: WKT outer-ring parse + ray-cast point-in-polygon + geofield bounding-box pre-filter; `syncHoa`/`syncAll`; cached `hoaForPoint()`. Hooks (`bos_hoa.module`): HOA insert/update re-syncs its boundary to member homes; residential **presave** auto-assigns the home in-memory (no recursion — `_bos_hoa_scan` guard). Drush **`bos:hoa:sync-members [--apply] [--hoa=ID]`**. 9/9 reversible dev tests (bbox compute, inside/outside, flag+link, release-on-undiscount). Deployed live (module enabled, `field_hoa` added, dry-run clean). **To activate an HOA it needs two data entries: (1) check "Discounted / Contracted HOA" on the HOA record, (2) a `field_boundary` polygon.** Boundary input today = paste WKT (draw on geojson.io / Google My Maps → export WKT) into the default geofield widget; an in-BOS draw-on-map widget (leaflet) is the pending UX choice. Setup: `setup_property_field_hoa.php`. Commit `e7bb282e`. **Boundary drawing (LIVE):** a Google Maps polygon-draw widget (`hoa_boundary_map`, `bos_hoa`) on the HOA edit page reuses BOS's existing `geofield_map` Google key — draw/edit the boundary on a map, round-trips to WKT (also accepts pasted WKT). `leaflet_widget` was rejected (dev-only + a second mapping stack). Commit `3acbda0e`. **Bear Creek pilot (LIVE):** boundary + Discounted flag set on Bear Creek HOA (144252); scan auto-flagged **6 member homes** (4 known + 2 discovered — Martin Animas St. Rental, Hale/Owl Creek Ave, confirmed Bear Creek) for the $60 discount (`seed_bearcreek_boundary.php`; rough rectangle, to be refined via the draw widget). **Phase 3** (public HOA showcase pages) remains. Docs: ROADMAP.

- **2026-09-07** — **HOA bundle = superset of property + reversible bundle converter + 14 HOAs converted (LIVE).** Follow-on to the Phase-1 HOA foundation. (1) Made the `hoa` bundle a **superset of `property`** — cloned all 23 remaining property field instances (+ form/view components) onto hoa, so HOAs have full property capability (contacts, zipcode/trip-fee, WO note, gate code, maps, flags, …) **plus** the 4 HOA extras (boundary, public desc, service dates) = 31 fields (`add_all_property_fields_to_hoa.php`). This makes a `property`→`hoa` conversion **lossless** (every field row maps to a field that exists on hoa). (2) **HOAs stay visible on crew lists** — crews service HOA common areas, so `teammate_properties` (crew list + map) + `properties_new` show all property types (an initial `exclude_hoa_from_property_lists.php` that hid them was **reversed** by `restore_hoa_in_property_lists.php` — same reasoning will apply to a future `commercial` bundle: no crew-list exclusion); admin `/admin/properties` keeps its exposed Type + Discounted filters so office can *optionally* isolate residential vs HOA; the **mow list is unaffected** (built on `property_lawn_maintenance`, bundle-agnostic, no property-type filter — a converted HOA with a mowing-contracted record stays on it). (3) New **reversible in-place bundle converter** `convert_property_bundle.php` — for the non-revisionable `properties` entity, updates `type` on base+data + `bundle` on all 30 field tables in a transaction, **keeping the same entity ID** so every WO/contract/ownership/detail-entity reference stays intact; snapshots + verifies ref counts before/after; re-saves to regenerate the title (hoa titles from `field_nickname`, property from address); dry-run default, swap FROM/TO to revert. Tested convert+revert on dev (all INTACT). (4) **Converted 14 real common-area records to `hoa` on live** (Bear Creek HOA + Retention Ponds, Crossroads Park + Sidewalks, West Mountain Ranch, Cherrywood Villas + Sidewalks, all 7 Stonebridge HOA records) — e.g. Cherrywood 527 WOs / Crossroads 499 / Stonebridge Main 430, **all references INTACT**, no crew-list leak, mow list unchanged, site 200. Live DB backed up first (`~/pre-hoa-convert-20260907.sql.gz`, 135M, verified). Fully reversible. Commits `676633a6` + earlier. Docs: ROADMAP, CLAUDE.md properties section.

- **2026-09-07** — **HOA / Common Area property type — Phase 1 foundation (LIVE).** New **`hoa` bundle on the `properties` entity** (chosen over a standalone entity to reuse the property page, map, photo gallery, and WO plumbing). Models an HOA common-area we maintain. Fields: `field_nickname` (HOA name, auto-labels the entity), `field_neighborhood`, `field_geofield` (center point), **`field_boundary`** (geofield **GPS polygon** — the basis for Phase-2 point-in-polygon auto-flagging of member homes), `field_hoa_contracted` (is this HOA discounted?), `field_hoa_public_desc` (public description), `field_service_start`/`field_service_end` (common-area service timeframe). `/admin/properties` list gains exposed **Type** (Property/HOA) + **Discounted (HOA)** filters — the discounted one is a **grouped** filter so it defaults to "Any" (a non-grouped boolean would have defaulted to value=1 and shown only discounted on load — caught + fixed in testing). Built via idempotent entity-API scripts (`setup_hoa_property_bundle.php`, `add_hoa_property_list_filters.php`; bundle via `properties_type` storage; auto_entitylabel `[properties:field_nickname]`). ECK/field/view configs deploy via the scripts (cim-skip). Deploy: scp scripts + run on live + `cr` (additive; no cim/DB migration). Verified live (2 bundles, list loads, filters present). **Chosen direction (Todd):** GPS boundary auto-detect for membership; foundation first. **Phase 2** = point-in-polygon auto-flag residential homes inside a discounted HOA boundary → sets `field_hoa_contracted` → winterizing HOA discount ($60). **Phase 3** = public HOA showcase pages (public view mode/route, before/after gallery, timeframe — "drive by and see the difference"). Commits `88e3f4da` (merge `653d661f`). Docs: ROADMAP.

- **2026-09-07** — **Winterizing pricing: $95 base + auto-applied promo discounts + HOA/neighborhood (LIVE).** Base winterizing fee **$90 → $95** (`config_pages:business_setting.field_winterizing_base_fee`). **4 new discount amounts** on Business Settings (new "Winterizing Discounts" group): `field_winterize_disc_contract` (signed contract / automatic list) **$5**, `field_winterize_disc_services` (contract with >4 services) **$10**, `field_winterize_disc_new_cust` (new customer, 4+ homes) **$15**, `field_winterize_disc_hoa` (HOA contracted) **$35**. **Applied automatically at winterizing sign-off** (`wo_sprinkler_winterizing`) so office staff can't miss a promoted rate — the **single LARGEST** applicable discount wins (they do **NOT** stack, per decision); amount + reason recorded on the WO (`field_winterize_discount` / `field_winterize_discount_reason`) and subtracted from `field_wo_total`. **Detection:** HOA = property flag; >4 services = current-year residential contract's selected-section count (`field_do_you_want IN 1,4`) > 4; auto-list = a current-year winterizing contract line OR a prior-season winterizing WO; new-customer = **office checkbox `field_new_customer_discount` on the WO** (per-WO → that year only; replaced an owner-account auto-proxy per office request). Net effect: brand-new one-off = $95; returning/auto-list = $90; 4+ services = $85; new 4+ homes = $80; HOA/Bear Creek = $60. **Property page:** new `field_neighborhood` (text) + `field_hoa_contracted` (boolean, the HOA-discount trigger); **Bear Creek signups** (`service_request.field_campaign` = `bearcreek*`) **auto-set both** via `bos_service_request` insert/update hook. **Public pages** driven by Business Settings so they never drift from billing: `/winterize` shows **$95**, `/winterize/bear-creek` shows **$60** (= 95 − 35 HOA, was hardcoded). **Backfilled 4 existing Bear Creek properties** (Spierer/Hocking/Frenze/Hoch) to HOA-contracted. Scope: **winterizing only**. 17/17 reversible dev tests (base, each discount, largest-only, checkbox). Deploy: rsync 2 modules + 3 setup scripts + Bear-Creek backfill + `cr` (no cim/DB migration); config_pages/ECK/field configs deploy via the setup scripts. Existing completed WOs frozen/unchanged. Setup: `web/scripts/setup_winterizing_discounts_business_setting.php`, `setup_property_neighborhood_hoa.php`, `setup_wo_winterize_discount_fields.php`; backfill `backfill_bearcreek_hoa.php`. Commits `22a9b6bd` / `82c0245a`. **⚠ SOP:** office now (a) sees discounts auto-apply on winterizing invoices, (b) ticks "New Customer 4+ Homes" on the WO when applicable, (c) checks "HOA Contracted" on a property to give the $60 rate. Docs: `Modules/wo_bundle_modules.md`.

- **2026-09-07** — **Sprinkler System Type on winterizing + start-up WOs — the pump-fee charge driver, now recorded on the WO (LIVE).** Winterizing/start-up billing charges a **base fee + $25 pump fee** when the system needs a pump (Dirty/Duel/Well), keyed on the **System Type** term (`sprinkler_system_types`: 13=Domestic base-only, 15=Dirty / 16=Duel / 30742=Well = base+pump; rates in `business_setting` — `field_winterizing_base_fee` $90 / `field_start_up_base_fee` $80 / shared `field_winterizing_pump_fee` $25). Problem: the WO **never stored** System Type — `wo_sprinkler_winterizing`/`wo_sprinkler_start_up` read the **property's** `field_system_type` **live at sign-off**, so it was invisible to crews, unqueryable per-WO ("how many had pumps?" required reaching back through the property), and any later property edit **silently re-priced** old WOs. Fix: added **`field_system_type`** (new shared `work_order.field_system_type` storage → `sprinkler_system_types`, radios) to **both** bundles and made the WO the source of truth — **(1) pre-fill** from the property's **primary (delta 0)** sprinkler system on WO create (presave, `isNew` + empty); **(2) bill off the WO's own value** — a crew correction on site **wins** — with property fallback, via `_wo_sw_resolve_system_type_total()` / `_wo_ssu_resolve_system_type_total()` → `field_task_rate` → `field_wo_total`; **(3) freeze** the resolved value onto the WO at completion so a later property change never re-prices it; **(4) Option A write-back** (`hook_entity_insert`/`update` → `_wo_sprinkler_*_write_back_system_type()`) pushes the WO's corrected System Type back onto the property's **single** sprinkler system on Complete (1097) so the property stays current for next year (skipped for 0 or >1 systems — don't guess). Multi-system properties (rare — 2 of 629 last year) keep the per-system property sum. **7/7 reversible dev tests** each (pre-fill, base+pump billing, freeze, override-wins, write-back, restore). **Backfilled 9,831 historical WOs** (winterizing + start-up) via **direct DB insert into `work_order__field_system_type`** (work_order is non-revisionable; revision_id=entity_id, langcode `en`) — **bypasses entity save so NO presave fires and NO billing is recomputed**; frozen totals untouched, only the new field populated (6,984 Domestic / 2,360 Dirty / 284 Duel / 203 Well; 301 had no system type on file → self-heals as future WOs complete + write back). Now a one-field query: **2025 completed winterizing = 205 pump / 405 domestic** (and 321 of 629 had >5 zones = 1,650 extra billable zones). Setup (entity-API, cim-skip): `web/scripts/setup_wo_winterizing_system_type.php`, `setup_wo_start_up_system_type.php`; backfill `backfill_wo_system_type.php` (dry-run default, `BOS_BACKFILL_APPLY=1`). Deploy: rsync 2 modules + 2 setup scripts + `cr` (no cim/DB migration); additive field-table INSERTs are trivially reversible (table was empty pre-backfill). Commits `6e92b1de` / `f29f4a74` / `663fc1bb` (merge `c9a1d772`). Docs: `Modules/wo_bundle_modules.md` ("System Type on winterizing/start-up"). **Note:** billing is unchanged for existing/new WOs **unless** a crew corrects System Type on site (the intended behavior); `sprinkler_start_up` estimate side already had the field — WO side now matches.

- **2026-09-06** — **Win-Back Phase 2 — call-intelligence capture + report (LIVE).** Perishable market intel captured at call time (callers started the list Thursday, most of 165 unworked). Each win-back card gets an optional, non-blocking **"Details"** panel: *done elsewhere last year?* (yes/no/unknown), *who did it?* (competitor free text), *why did they leave?* (price / too-late / service / moved-sold / no-longer / other + free text). Saved via AJAX (`bos_winback.intel`, POST+CSRF) → `WinbackListService::captureIntel()` **merges into the same KeyValue call record** as the outcome buttons (`bos_winback.call_state`, keyed `year:pid`) — reconciled, not a parallel store. New **Report tab** (`bos_winback.report`, `WinbackListService::intelReport()`) rolls the season up by **why-they-left** + **who-did-it-instead (competitors)** + done-elsewhere counts — a **controller** report (not Views) because the data is KeyValue, not entity/field-queryable. Tabs added (`links.task.yml`: Call list · Report). `WinbackListService::ELSEWHERE`/`WHY_LEFT` constants. Deploy: rsync module + `cr` (no cim/DB). Verified live (routes, list 165 rows + 6 options, capture→report round-trip). Also earlier today: **backfilled `react26` attribution** for the 32 last-week win-back winterizing WOs (`web/scripts/backfill_winback_attribution.php`; 4 web + 1 genuine new-customer correctly skipped) → Report shows `react26 | Reactivation | 32`. **⚠ SOP:** callers now have Create-WO one-click + auto-enroll + the Details capture — a short training note covers the whole win-back calling flow.

- **2026-09-06** — **Win-Back button attribution + phone auto-enroll — Phase 1 (LIVE).** The win-back list's **Create WO** was a plain link to the WO add form; it now **creates a `service_request`** (`sprinkler_winterizing`) stamped `field_campaign=react{YY}` (prefix from `bos_winback.settings.campaign_prefix`, default `react`, + 2-digit year appended and **stored once, never recomputed** — a 2026 booking still reads `react26` in 2027), `field_source=reactivation`, authored by the **caller** (free per-caller productivity), then **converts it to a WO via the existing `ServiceRequestConverter`** (`bos_service_request.converter`). **BOS-aligned decision:** attribution lives on `service_request.field_campaign` (same field as web `?c=` codes) — NOT a new `work_order` field — so win-back bookings appear in the **same campaign→jobs Report** as web bookings (verified: `react26 | Reactivation`), no parallel system. **First-touch preserved automatically** — a property with a current booking is off the win-back list AND the converter's eligibility gate refuses the duplicate (a caller can't steal credit from an `fb26` ad). **"Add to auto email list" checkbox** → sets the Contact service-email opt-in + `field_consent_source=phone` + `field_consent_updated=now` + a `consent_log` row (actor+timestamp, no IP — correct for a phone call) via `bos_consent_log`; **never flips an existing opt-out** (leaves it, tells the caller). New `bos_winback.create` route (POST, CSRF header token, mirrors the `/mark` AJAX pattern); reuses `winback.js` `csrfToken()`; opens the created WO to finish details. `WinbackController::createBooking()` (NOT `create()` — that's the DI factory), `WinbackListService::contactForProperty()`. Deps added: `bos_service_request`, `bos_consent_log`. Deploy: rsync module + `drush cset bos_winback.settings campaign_prefix react` + `cr` (no cim/DB). Verified live (route, deps, list 165 rows, config, react26 allowlisted). **Phase 2 (not built):** call-capture fields (why they left / who did it instead / outcome) + a "why they left" view — the "outcome" field must reconcile with the existing win-back call-state buttons, not duplicate them. **⚠ SOP:** callers' Create WO flow changed (now one-click creates the booking + the opt-in checkbox) — worth a training note. **Next season:** add `react27` (+ `goog27` etc.) to the campaign allowlist (config, no deploy).

- **2026-09-06** — **Shorter centered header + Fractus wordmark rolled to winterize/fall-cleanup/homepage (LIVE).** (1) **Header height:** cut Olivero's tall default header (`--site-header-height-wide` = `--sp10` = **180px**, content bottom-aligned) down to **96px** with the logo centered. The 180px is a `min-height` **spacer** and IS overridable — but shrinking it alone repeatedly "lost the hamburger" because **four Olivero constants are interlocked** and must scale together: `--site-header-height-wide` (header), `.sticky-header-toggle { height: var(--sp6) }` (**108px** toggle tab — clips if taller than the header), `.site-header__fixable`/`.site-header__initial` `align-items: flex-end` (bottom-piles content + the X), and the fixed-state `inset-block-start: calc(… − var(--sp4))` (**72px** upward pull that clipped the short header on scroll). Override all four in `bo-brand.css` (desktop `≥75rem` only; mobile hamburger breakpoint untouched): height 6rem, toggle 6rem, both fixable+initial `center`, and drop the −sp4 pull-up (`inset-block-start: var(--drupal-displace-offset-top,0)`). Sticky-collapse + toggle still work. (2) **Wordmark rollout:** the Fractus "Brookstone" artwork (from the header work) now replaces the Oswald "BROOKSTONE" in the winterize + fall-cleanup **topbars** (ink) and all three **footers** (winterize/fall-cleanup/homepage — white version on the ink footers); shared `.bo-wordmark` rule + flex/baseline on `.bo-topbar__name`/`.bo-footer__name`/`.home-footer__name`. Deploy: rsync theme css/templates + homepage module template/css + `cr` (no cim/DB). Verified live. **Reusable rule:** to resize the Olivero header, scale those four constants in concert — or (bulletproof) disable the sticky-collapse and set a plain height. See `Governance/drupal_bos_gotchas.md`.

- **2026-09-06** — **Header wordmark "Brookstone" in the logo font (Fractus) — LIVE, as licensed artwork.** The word "Brookstone" in the header lockup now uses the actual logo font (**Fractus Normal**), while "Outdoors" stays Oswald. **Licensing guardrail:** the Fractus TTF is **`fsType = 2` (Restricted — no embedding)** — read straight from the font's OS/2 table (pure-PHP/Python parse; the file was never committed/served). So it is NOT a webfont; instead the word ships as a **transparent PNG exported from the licensed desktop font** (`brookstone_olivero/images/brookstone-wordmark.png`, ink; `+-white.png` spare) — normal desktop use (same as making the logo), no font file exposed. Header template swaps the `BROOKSTONE` text for the image + keeps `Outdoors` as Oswald text, baseline-aligned (`.bo-brand__name` → flex, `.bo-brand__wordmark { height:1.02em }`). Always on the sand bar (the ink slide-reveal is behind, so no light-version swap needed). Deploy: rsync theme template/css/images + `cr` (no cim/DB). Verified live. **Follow-up (offered):** roll the same wordmark into the winterize/fall-cleanup marketing pages, homepage, and footers for consistency. **Reusable rule:** check a font's `fsType` before any webfont use; if restricted, render the word to artwork (PNG/SVG) rather than embedding the file.

- **2026-09-06** — **Site favicon / app icons from the Brookstone emblem (LIVE).** The active themes shipped **no favicon** (browser tab showed the default Drupal drop). Generated a full icon set from the round emblem (`bo-logo-round.png`, 1562² transparent) with ImageMagick (`ddev exec convert`; ImageMagick is in the DDEV web container, not on the host): `favicon.ico` (16/32/48), `favicon-16x16.png`, `favicon-32x32.png`, `apple-touch-icon.png` (180, flattened on white for a clean home-screen tile), `android-chrome-192/512`, + `site.webmanifest` — in `bos_service_request/assets/icons/`. Wired **site-wide** (public + admin + login, every theme) via `bos_service_request_page_attachments` (the module already publishes brand tokens sitewide) using `html_head_link` (icon/apple-touch/manifest); `bos_service_request_page_attachments_alter` **removes the core default** `/core/misc/favicon.ico` so only the emblem is advertised (core adds its favicon in `system_page_attachments`, so removal must be in `_alter`, not preprocess_html). Deploy: rsync icons + module + `cr` (additive; no cim/DB). Verified live (only Brookstone icon links, all files 200). Source emblem already high-res in-repo, so no upload needed.

- **2026-09-06** — **Campaign codes + channel source mapping for paid ads (LIVE).** Prep for the ~$1,200 Meta/Google paid push. **(1) Allowlist:** added the paid `?c=` codes to `bos_service_request.settings.campaigns` so winterize/fall-cleanup bookings store them (non-allowlisted codes file as `unknown`): `fb26, fb26rt, fb26-nbr, fbo26, goog26, goog26-nbr, grp26, door26, react26` (join existing `website, pc26/a/b, bearcreek26, online26, goog26-fall`). Script `web/scripts/add_campaign_codes.php` (idempotent, `BOS_CAMPAIGN_CODES=…`, active-config edit, per-env). `resolveCampaign` matches **exact/case-sensitive** (`in_array(...,TRUE)`), so codes must appear in the ad URL verbatim (lowercase, hyphens preserved). **Note:** analytics/ads attribution works for ANY code regardless (bos_analytics reads the raw `?c=`); the allowlist only governs the BOS booking's `field_campaign`. `<hood>26` is a template — each neighborhood code is added as it forms. **(2) Source mapping:** new shared `Drupal\bos_service_request\CampaignSource::forCode()` maps a code → a meaningful `field_source` (Google Ads / Meta paid / Meta organic / Community Group / Door Hanger / Reactivation / Online Promo / Neighborhood / Postcard QR / Website) instead of the old blanket `postcard_qr`(winterize)/`other`(fall-cleanup). `field_source` is a **constrained list** — its `allowed_values` were extended FIRST (`web/scripts/setup_campaign_source_values.php`, idempotent, no cim) so a booking can never fail to save; unmapped codes (e.g. a not-yet-mapped hood) safely fall to `other`. Both forms' `resolveCampaign` now call the shared mapper (WinterizeForm, FallCleanupForm). Deploy order (dev→live): rsync mapper + add allowed values **before** the form code, then rsync forms + `cr`. Verified live (mapping + values + `/winterize` 200). **Follow-up:** when a neighborhood cluster forms, add its code to both the allowlist (add_campaign_codes) and `CampaignSource` exact-map (→ `neighborhood`); internal `source` label for a campaign code still resolves via the mapper (the legacy `postcard_qr` default is retired for these).

- **2026-09-06** — **Analytics — Google Tag Manager + no-PII conversion events — `bos_analytics` (LIVE).** BOS had **zero analytics/pixels** before this (verified 2026-09-05). New custom module injects a **GTM container** (the single tag host) on **every page including `/user/login`** — head loader (`hook_page_attachments`, weight −1000) + `<noscript>` (`hook_page_top`); passive snippet, auth/sessions/login flow untouched. Container ID from **`settings.php`** `$settings['bos_gtm_container_id']` (env-specific, off-git, format-validated; renders nothing when unset → **dev stays untracked** unless the line is added; live = `GTM-WC8F8PCN`). **Conversion events:** `hook_form_alter` appends a capture submit handler to the public forms (**live form classes NOT edited**) → on success records a one-shot session flag → `dataLayer.push` on the confirmation render / next request: **`winterize_submit`** (`winterize_done`), **`cleanup_submit`** (`fc_done`), **`portal_waitlist_submit`** (redirect). Payload is **`{event, campaign}` only** (normalized `?c=`; **no PII**); the push is self-guarding + async and the capture handler is `try/catch`-wrapped and runs after the record saves, so it **cannot block or break a form**; interim/partial saves don't fire. **GA4 (`G-BGR1SF7LTM`), the Meta Pixel, and Google Ads conversion are all configured inside GTM's web UI — not in BOS code** (so new tags need no code deploy); GTM triggers on the three custom events. Verified live: GTM loader + noscript on homepage/`/winterize`/`/user/login`, site 200; dataLayer event tested (fires only on success, one-shot, event+campaign only, no PII). Deploy: rsync module → `settings.php` line → `drush en bos_analytics` → `cr` (additive; no cim/DB). Docs: `Modules/bos_analytics.md`. **Owed (Todd/browser):** build the 3 tags in GTM (`tagmanager.google.com`); browser verification (GTM Preview / Meta Pixel Helper / GA4 DebugView / end-to-end `?c=fb26`); no web analytics history predates this.

- **2026-09-06** — **Customer-portal waitlist on the login page — `bos_portal_waitlist` (LIVE).** The login page (`/user/login`) is now two columns: **crew/office login (unchanged) on the left**, a **"Coming for customers" waitlist panel on the right** (stacked on mobile, login first, with a mobile-only jump link). Captures interest in a **future** customer portal (product-validation signal) + opt-ins — **not** an account signup (no user/password/auth). `hook_form_user_login_form_alter` only adds the "Crew & Office Login" heading + jump link (fields/validation/route/reset untouched); CSS-only two-column via a `bo-login-page` body class flexing `.region--content` (`css/waitlist.css`); default "Log in" page title hidden. **`PortalWaitlistForm`** (reCAPTCHA + flood 10/hr-IP, 3/day-email; campaign `?c=` default `portal26`) writes to **Contact** (the consent home): `field_opt_in_service_email` **on submit** (submitting = a request to be emailed when accounts open), `field_opt_in_marketing_email` only on the seasonal tick, `field_opt_in_service_sms` only on the scheduling tick **+** a mobile, `field_consent_updated`/`field_consent_source=web_form` when a flag changed — every change logged by `bos_consent_log` (source web_form, IP captured). **Portal interest is a product signal, NOT consent** — plain new fields `field_portal_interest`/`_date`/`_note` (mobile/address/already-customer/campaign in the note), deliberately never in the consent_log. **Governance guards beyond the spec:** multi-signal Contact matching (email → last-name+phone) to avoid duplicate person records now that fabricated Contact emails are cleared; opt-out guard (never flip an existing `opted_out`, logs a notice); fabricated-email override (submitted real address replaces a stored `@ourdomain` one — **client User email never touched**, per `client_email_policy.md`). Fields + block via idempotent entity-API scripts (`setup_portal_interest_fields.php`, `setup_portal_waitlist_block.php`) — **no cim**, additive only. Deploy: rsync module+scripts → field script → `drush en bos_portal_waitlist` → block script → `cr`. Verified live (panel + 4 bullets render, captcha present, login form intact, 3 fields present). Docs: `Modules/bos_portal_waitlist.md`. **Owed:** ⚠ SOP (office handling of waitlist leads when portal ships); no web analytics in BOS (login-button→submit funnel unmeasured — add before a paid campaign); the future "accounts are open" email MUST carry the send-time consent check (doesn't exist yet); confirm/curate the 4 portal-promise bullets before heavy traffic.

- **2026-09-05** — **`sewardslandscape.com` purged from client User emails — domain rewrite (LIVE).** Confirmed we **no longer own `sewardslandscape.com`** (mail there reaches a third party) and that **`brookstoneoutdoors.com` will have a catch-all**. The Contact-side fakes were already cleared (below), but **1,990 client User accounts (1,887 active) still held `@sewardslandscape.com` emails** — a third-party exposure via core Drupal mail (password reset / admin "notify"). Per Todd, applied a **sanctioned one-time exception** to client-email-policy part 1 (for the un-owned domain only): `web/scripts/rewrite_seward_user_domain.php` (idempotent, dry-run default, domain-guarded) rewrote **only the domain** `@sewardslandscape.com` → `@brookstoneoutdoors.com` on all 1,990 accounts — **local part / name preserved**; 5 needed `<local>.<uid>@` disambiguation to stay unique; programmatic `User::save()` sends no mail on a mail change. After: **0** `@sewardslandscape.com` anywhere (User or Contact), 2,895 users `@brookstoneoutdoors.com`, **0** duplicate user emails, site 200. Still fabricated (never a customer-send target) but now under our catch-all → can't leave our control. Deploy: DB dump (`~/pre-seward-rewrite-20260905.sql.gz`, 135M) → rsync → dry-run (dev+live) → apply → `cr`. Doc: `__BOS_AI/Governance/client_email_policy.md` (residual exposure → RESOLVED).

- **2026-09-05** — **Fabricated Contact emails cleared + client-email policy recorded (LIVE).** An audit found **~78% of customer-linked contacts (2,734 of 3,519) carry a fabricated our-domain email** (`name@brookstoneoutdoors.com` / `@sewardslandscape.com` — built from the person's name at client-account creation, before the Contact entity existed); only **~780 (~22%)** are real/deliverable. Most fabricated ones (1,860) are at the legacy `sewardslandscape.com`. **Recorded policy** `__BOS_AI/Governance/client_email_policy.md`: (1) a client User's email changes **only by admin-manual edit** — no automated replace; (2) customer-facing sends **skip** our-domain/placeholder addresses (a send needs BOTH opted-in AND a real external address); (3) **never delete** client User records/emails (they're the login/identity anchor + the `customer_profile.field_primary_contact_ref` link). **Then cleared the Contact-side fakes:** `web/scripts/clear_fabricated_contact_emails.php` (idempotent, dry-run default, **domain-guarded** so it can only ever remove our-domain values) deleted `field_email` on the 2,734 fabricated `contacts.contact` records — **values only**; records/names/phones/opt-in flags/titles (title is name+phone) and all customer↔Contact links retained; **client User emails untouched**; 9 staff mailboxes + 1 placeholder protected. After: 3,690 contacts unchanged, ~787 still hold an email (real + placeholders), 10 @our-domain remain (the protected staff/placeholder), 2 real service opt-ins intact, site 200. Contacts are **not** on ownership_records and `field_email` isn't required, so clearing is safe. Read-only audit (`docs/email-audit-detail-20260905.csv`, gitignored — PII) + review lists delivered to Todd. Deploy: DB dump (`~/pre-clear-emails-20260905.sql.gz`, 135M) → rsync script → dry-run (dev+live) → apply → `cr`. **Open for Todd:** is there a catch-all on brookstoneoutdoors.com; do we still own sewardslandscape.com (1,860 fabricated addresses pointed there). Related: the consent model below.

- **2026-09-05** — **Contact opt-in/consent model + append-only consent log (LIVE).** Established the person-vs-account split: **Contact = the person + opt-ins only (no BOS access, nothing sensitive)**; **User + `customer_profile` keep ALL secure/business data unchanged** (role-gated); `customer_profile.field_primary_contact_ref` is the spine linking them. **Stage 1** — six fields on `contacts.contact`: split opt-in booleans **`field_opt_in_marketing_email` / `_marketing_sms` / `_service_email` / `_service_sms`** (marketing vs service/transactional × channel — a legacy vague "OK to email" is never treated as marketing consent), plus `field_consent_updated` (datetime; **NULL = never asked/unknown**, which marketing sends MUST treat as not-opted-in) and `field_consent_source` (web_form/phone/paper/import/staff). **Consent log** — new append-only **`consent_log`** ECK entity + **`bos_consent_log`** module: one immutable row per flag change (`field_contact`, channel, consent_type, `field_old_state`→`field_new_state` unknown/opted_in/opted_out, event_source incl. `system`, actor, IP, note, created ts) — written by the SAME save that changes the flag (`hook_entity_presave` detects the diff via `loadUnchanged` + stamps the cache; insert/update writes the rows), so the log can't drift from the booleans; edits refused on every path (`bos_consent_log_consent_log_presave` throws unless new). Attribution via hints on the contact (`$c->_consent_source/_consent_actor/_consent_ip/_consent_note`; default `staff` if authenticated, `web_form` if anon). View perm to office/ops roles only. **Stage 3 backfill** — `customer_profile.field_primary_contact_ref` was only 82% populated; match-by-email-or-create-and-link so every account has an opt-in home: **live 651 unlinked → 37** (9 linked to existing contacts + 605 created; **37 manual remainder** = 11 profiles with no valid user + 26 users whose name isn't a usable person name). Created contacts carry NO opt-ins (creating a record isn't a consent event). **Stage 2** — migrated the near-empty legacy User consent flags onto the linked Contact **conservatively as SERVICE consent only** (2 users → 3 consent_log rows, all `src=import`; marketing left 0). **Cleanup** — retired `field_ok_to_email` / `field_sms_consent` / `field_consent_updated` from User (kept operational `field_do_not_schedule` / `field_credit_hold` / `field_service_suspension_reason`), leaving Contact as the single consent home. Discovery ([`docs/contact-model-discovery.md`](docs/contact-model-discovery.md)) also found: JSON:API is read-only + anon can't read Property/WO/Contact (only `property_backflow_device`); all 11,911 files are public-scheme; BOS emails **no customers today** (all mail internal) — so send-time consent enforcement is greenfield. All via idempotent entity-API setup scripts (`setup_contact_consent_fields.php`, `setup_consent_log_entity.php`, `backfill_contact_primary_links.php`, `migrate_user_consent_to_contact.php`, `retire_user_consent_fields.php`; dry-run defaults, no PII in output) — **no cim**. Deploy: DB dump (`~/pre-consent-model-20260905.sql.gz`, 136M) → rsync module+scripts → Stage 1 → create consent_log → `drush en bos_consent_log` → Stage 3 dry-run+apply → Stage 2 → cleanup → `cr`. Verified live (6/6 fields, 37 unlinked, 3 log rows `src=import`, User fields gone, front 200). Plan: [`docs/contact-consent-plan.md`](docs/contact-consent-plan.md). **Follow-ups:** office reviews the 37 unlinked profiles + 2 unmigrated consents; intake forms write opt-in to the Contact (`_consent_source='web_form'`); send-time consent check when a customer-facing mail path is built; optional office View over `consent_log`.

- **2026-09-05** — **Public homepage — new `bos_homepage` module + sitewide brand header (LIVE).** Replaced the login-screen front page with a real 6-band public homepage (hero, trust strip, seasonal promo, services, proof/portfolio, how-it-works, who-we-are, careers) built to the approved spec. **Module `bos_homepage`:** `HomepageController::page()` + `_bos_homepage_render_array()` feed the `bos_homepage` theme (`templates/bos-homepage.html.twig`); all copy is editable via `HomepageSettingsForm` (`config/install/bos_homepage.settings.yml`); `RequestEstimateForm` creates an `estimate_request` (cascades to estimate + contact via `estimate_intake`); `PhoneBlock` renders the SVG phone icon (via `Markup::create` — `#markup` runs `Xss::filterAdmin` and strips `svg`/`path`); `PortfolioImportCommands` (`drush bos_homepage:portfolio-import`) folder-drives the Proof band (hidden until ≥4 photos). `hook_page_attachments` emits **LocalBusiness JSON-LD** on the front route; `hook_block_access` hides `page_title_block` on front (hero H1 is the only H1 — the account "Log in" block is **kept** so the homepage header matches every other page). **Full-bleed bands:** `page--front.html.twig` renders `{{ bos_home }}` directly in `<main>` (bypassing Olivero's constrained content region — same approach as `/winterize`); `.page-wrapper--front{max-width:none}`; content capped by a single `--home-content: 1040px` var. **Sitewide brand header:** `block--system-branding-block.html.twig` = the winterize-style lockup (round emblem + BROOKSTONE OUTDOORS + tagline); sand header background + centering + black slide-reveal + bolder blue Services caret in `brookstone_olivero/css/bo-brand.css`; header height left at Olivero default (hamburger reliability — no geometry overrides). Mobile polish (rounds M1–M5): one-line header phone, no hyphenation/justify, hero scrim, centered trust bullets, 3-line town list without orphan bullets. **Footer:** round logo beside the name + dynamic-year copyright (`© {{ "now"|date("Y") }}`) — same treatment applied to the winterize/fall-cleanup footers. Seasonal promo button centered + color-reversed (white bg, orange text). Setup: `web/scripts/setup_homepage.php` (page.front → /home; creates /careers; places phone blocks) + `drush en bos_homepage`; **no cim**; login route/auth untouched. Deploy: rsync module + theme + `cr`. Docs: `Modules/bos_homepage.md`. **Follow-ups:** office stages ≥4 portfolio photos on live then run the import; hero image is a module asset (UI image field is a possible later add).

- **2026-09-04** — **Public service-request intake — Gate 6: Fall Cleanup landing + `fall_cleanup` bundle + admin card queue (LIVE).** Second public intake bundle on `bos_service_request` (after `sprinkler_winterizing`). **(1) Fall Cleanup landing page** at the Fall Cleanup services term (`/services/landscape-lawn-care/yard-cleanup/fall-cleanup`, Google Ads `?c=goog26-fall`) — public/client viewers get the marketing page (`page--fall-cleanup.html.twig`, breadcrumb + editable "How we do it" body from the term's `field_service_public_desc`), internal roles get the normal crew-training term page. `FallCleanupForm` intake mirrors `WinterizeForm` (reCAPTCHA + flood, §6.0 no-property-disclosure). **(2) Cross-sell** (reduce office "forgetting"): Winterize box → a linked winterize service-request; Spring-landscape box → auto `estimate_request` design-build lead (deliberately cascades to estimate + contact via `estimate_intake`); Snow box → a review flag. **(3) Admin queue → cards:** `/admin/office/service-requests` (`service_request_admin` view) converted from a table to the BOS status-card pattern — created-WO link, resolved contact (phone/email), a **status exposed filter** and bundle (type) filter, with Edit/actions moved to the right under the status badge (`views_view_fields__service_request_admin` + `sr_card` hooks, `request-cards.css`, `views-view-fields--service-request-admin.html.twig`). Backfilled 4 unlinked requests to their WOs (`web/scripts/backfill_service_request_wo_links.php`); flagged the Approve-button retrain SOP. Deploy: rsync module + setup scripts (fall_cleanup bundle/fields) + `cr`; no cim. Docs: `Modules/bos_service_request.md`. Abstraction proven — further public bundles are now cheap.

- **2026-09-04** — **Snow contract P4 — depth-tiered plow billing (LIVE).** Wired the tiered plow rates into `wo_snow_removal` billing. Standardized the `snow_levels` vocab to **0-2" / 2-4" / 4-6" / 6"+ / Icy** with `field_snow_depth_tier` (`web/scripts/standardize_snow_levels.php`, `setup_snow_depth_tier_field.php`); new `get_snow_depth_tier_for_wo()` + `_wo_snow_removal_latest_contract()` + `get_snow_plow_rate_for_wo()` in `wo_snow_removal.module` map a WO's recorded snow depth → the matching contract tier rate (`field_plow_rate_0_2` / `_2_4` / `_4_6` / `_6_plus`), **falling back to the flat `field_per_push_rate`** when the tier rate is empty (so nothing mis-bills before the office fills the tier rates). - **2026-09-03** — **Snow contract P3 — agreement workflow toolbar + signed-PDF + hard version-lock (LIVE).** `contract_snow` gains the office workflow toolbar on the snow contract page (Preview / Mark Sent / Upload Signed / Activate) via `SnowContractActionsForm`, a signed-PDF upload field, and a **hard version-lock**: once a contract is Executed/Active a presave guard freezes its pricing + terms (template version stamped) so the signed agreement can't drift. - **2026-09-02** — **Snow Removal Service Agreement — PDF P2 (LIVE).** 2-page agreement PDF at **`/contracts/snow/{contract}/agreement`** (`contract_snow`, office-role-gated), rendered via **entity_print + dompdf** — same stack as the backflow report (`SnowAgreementController` → `renderInIsolation` the `snow_agreement` Twig template → dompdf `getBlob()` → inline `Response`). **Page 1:** branded header (bo-logo-round.png data-URI), customer/property (resolved from `field_property` → primary contact), service method + trigger, the **4 customer-facing pricing tiers** (0–2 / 2–4 / 4–6 / 6"+, from the new `field_plow_rate_*`) with the proportional/multi-service billing language, sidewalk + ice-control (salt/mag rates), property instructions. **Page 2:** the standard Terms & Conditions (2026–2027 season, snow-depth, timing, on-call, ice control, property/gravel, billing 1.5%/mo, cancellation, acceptance) + signature area.
