Deterministic; draft is streamed, not stored. **Does NOT expose internal WO fields** (1/4-plow etc.). Sample generator `web/scripts/gen_snow_agreement_sample.php`; sample sent to Todd for visual review. Deploy: rsync module + `cr` (route rebuild; no cim/DB). **Next:** P3 = workflow (Preview/Generate/Mark Sent/Upload Signed/Activate) + version-lock on execution + signed-PDF upload field; P4 = wire tiered rates into `wo_snow_removal` billing (depth → tier).

- **2026-09-02** — **Snow Removal Service Agreement — contract build-out P1 (LIVE).** The snow contract (`contracts.snow_removal`) was ~greenfield (3 records, no module). **(1) Tiered plow pricing:** broke the single `field_per_push_rate` into depth tiers **`field_plow_rate_0_2` / `_2_4` / `_4_6` / `_6_plus`** (decimal(10,2), $; the legacy single rate is kept — `wo_snow_removal` billing still uses it until the tiered-billing phase). **(2) New `contract_snow` module** (mirrors `contract_residential`): assigns a stable **contract number `SNOW-{year}-{id}`** on insert (double-save, AEL-style) + defaults **template version v1.0**. **(3) P1 fields** for the agreement: `field_snow_contract_number`, `field_snow_service_method` (Automatic/On-Call), `field_snow_trigger` (in), `field_snow_ice_authorized`, `field_salt_rate`, `field_mag_rate`, `field_shovel_rate`, `field_snow_property_instructions`, `field_snow_template_version` (+ `field_requires_ice_treatment` from the Phase-2 snow-WO work). All placed on the default + admin contract forms + default view. Entity-API scripts (`setup_snow_tiered_plow_rates.php`, `setup_snow_contract_fields.php`) + sync YAMLs (ECK/field skip cim). Field name cap gotcha: `field_snow_ice_control_authorized` (33) exceeded the 32-char limit → `field_snow_ice_authorized`. **BOS already has the PDF stack the agreement needs** — `entity_print`+`dompdf` (the backflow test-report precedent), `endroid/qr-code`, `bo-tokens.css` — so **P2** = the 2-page Snow Removal Service Agreement Twig+print-CSS template + QR (contract number), **P3** = workflow (Preview/Generate/Mark Sent/Upload Signed/Activate) + version-lock on execution + signed-PDF upload field, **P4** = wire tiered rates into billing. Verified on live (auto-number, all 14 fields). Deploy: rsync module + field scripts + `drush en contract_snow` + `cr` (no cim/DB).

- **2026-09-02** — **Snow Removal WO cleanup + completion validation (LIVE).** **(1) "How many times(pushes)" field cleanup** (`wo_snow_removal` form_alter on `wo_tasks_list.snow_removal`): the field is an entity-ref to the **`snow_plows`** vocab (billing = contract `field_per_push_rate` × the term's `field_calculation_factor`). Form now offers **only the real plow-portion terms** (factor > 0: 1/4, 1/2, 3/4, 1 Complete, 2 Complete) — the legacy **"Did Not Plow"** (factor 0, **used by 216 WOs**) + **"Helped Plow"** (1) terms and the empty **"N/A"** radio are dropped from the form; **terms are NOT deleted** (historical refs + factors intact), and a record already holding a dropped term still shows it on edit (won't wipe). Field now **only shows when "Snow Plowed" is checked** (`#states`, visibility only — never blocks a save). **(2) Phase 2 — completion validation** (`wo_sign_off`, mirrors the lawn_mowing Phase 2c pattern: form validate for a clean inline error + presave `EntityStorageException` backstop, gated on the `field_completed` cascade so interim saves aren't blocked): blocks **completion** of a snow WO when **Snow Plowed = Yes but no plow portion recorded**, or when **Snow Level is Icy AND the property's snow contract requires ice treatment but no Salt(lbs)/Mag(gal) amount > 0**. Icy detected by term name (survives the Phase 3 rename). New per-contract flag **`contracts.snow_removal.field_requires_ice_treatment`** (boolean, default off — office opts in per property, e.g. Walmart; entity-API script + sync YAMLs; placed on the contract form/view). Salt/Mag **amounts** already existed (`field_pounds_of_salt`, `field_snow_mag_gallons`). Tested reversibly on DDEV + live (6 scenarios). Deploy: rsync + field script + `cr` (no cim/DB). **Deferred (needs Todd's go):** Phase 3 = standardize the `snow_levels` terms to the 6 tiers + pricing map + historical term migration; require pushes/salt at completion is done, contract pricing engine not built. Test utils: `web/scripts/test_snow_completion_validation.php`, `setup_requires_ice_treatment_field.php`.

- **2026-09-01** — **Equipment auto-title + `equipment_types` name fixes — new `equipment_labels` module (LIVE).** Two `auto_entitylabel` gaps surfaced while adding a Husqvarna K770 (`power_tools`): (1) **`power_tools` has no `auto_entitylabel` config** (only heavy_equipment/small_engine/snow_plows/trailers/vehicles do) — and no `field_equipment_number` field — so new records saved **blank titles**; (2) **`equipment_types` auto-labels from `[term:field_common_name]`**, so a term created with only `name` set saved **blank** (the documented 2026-08-01 gotcha). New **`equipment_labels`** module (generic `hook_entity_presave`): for equipment bundles **without** an active `auto_entitylabel` pattern, composes the title from `field_equipment_make + field_equipment_type`(term common name) `+ field_model + " - " + field_equipment_number` (trailing `" -"` matches the fleet convention) — **only when the title is empty**, so clear+save regenerates and bundles WITH a pattern are left to `auto_entitylabel` (skip decided by reading **live** config → no conflict, no sync-drift risk); for `equipment_types` terms, copies `name → field_common_name` when the latter is empty, forced to run **before** `auto_entitylabel` via `hook_module_implements_alter` so the typed name survives the rebuild (works for form + programmatic creation). Tested reversibly on **DDEV + live** (term name preserved + common_name filled; power title auto-built; clear+save regenerates; records cleaned up). Deploy: rsync module + `drush en equipment_labels` + `cr` (no cim/DB). Resolves the manual title/name patching the 2nd Brain had been doing. Test util: `web/scripts/test_equipment_labels.php`.

- **2026-08-30** — **Scheduling Route Editor (Stages 2–5) + winterize date-rule correction + carry-forward fix (LIVE).** New map-backed **Route Editor** (`bos_scheduling`, supervisor-gated; moved to `/admin/office/work-orders/scheduling/route-editor` on 2026-09-30 — it had **no menu link at all**, contrary to the "Office → Calendar" claimed here) — a Google-map view of scheduled sprinkler WOs over a Day/3-Day/Week window to see and fix crew routing. **Stage 2** (read-only): stops plotted from `properties.field_geofield`, one route line per (day, tech) in `field_scheduled_oder` sequence, **Color by Day↔Crew** toggle (Day view defaults to Crew — distinct color per teammate), toggleable legend, no-location bucket, hover card↔marker link, tz-safe (raw `field_date` ts → PHP `America/Denver`, never `FROM_UNIXTIME`). **Stage 3 — crew assignment:** select stops (row checkboxes / per-route "select all") → **Assign** to a crew member or Unassign, one batched CSRF-guarded save; each save goes through the normal path so `wo_schedule` writes the "Re-assigned to …" audit note; **notify suppressed** (no email blast). **Stage 4 — drag-to-reorder:** native HTML5 drag (grip handle) within one route → writes `field_scheduled_oder` 1..N (not wo_schedule-tracked, so no note spam; notify suppressed); map redraws in place (viewport preserved). **Stage 5 — Optimize:** per-route button, greedy nearest-neighbor from the shop origin (client-side haversine, no paid API), persists via the reorder endpoint. Endpoints: `route_editor` / `_data` / `_assign` / `_reorder` (assign+reorder POST + `_csrf_request_header_token`). Origin = `bos_scheduling.settings` `route_origin_property_id` (default 50413). **Winterize date correction:** the earlier carry-forward used **same-nth-weekday-of-month**, which preserved each customer's weekday but **scrambled route order** year-to-year (Gerald Reeves' 1st-Wednesday customers leapfrogged from Oct 1 to Oct 7). Per Todd's rule, switched to the **calendar-date rule** — each stop keeps last year's **month/day** (lands one weekday later; season keeps its sequence + pace), weekend rule **Sat & Sun → next Monday** (crews work Mon–Fri). **Re-dated 443 of 459 already-applied 2026 records on live** (`web/scripts/winterize_redate_apply.php`, dry-run-gated; 16 new-customers w/o prior source left as-is; 0 errors; each logged a Rescheduled note; no emails; DB backed up to `~/pre-winterize-redate-20260830.sql.gz`). Verified: Gerald's Wednesday group back on Oct 1 (Thu), company-wide 0 Sundays / 1 Saturday (a no-source new customer). **Fixed the carry-forward command** (`WinterizeCarryForwardCommands`) to use the calendar-date rule permanently (removed `nthWeekday()`; `weekend_roll` flag auto-Mondays; only office closures now hold for review) so 2027+ is right. Deployed via rsync + `cr` (Route Editor on branch `feature/scheduling-route-editor`; no cim/DB migration). **"Route order set" carry-forward:** new dedicated boolean **`field_route_order_set`** on `scheduling.work_order` (label "Route order set" — **distinct from `field_scheduled_firm`, which is the customer-commitment "we told them a day" flag; never reuse that**) is **auto-stamped TRUE** whenever the office arranges a route in the Route Editor (drag-reorder or Optimize — both post to `reorder()`), shown as a green "✓ order set" badge. The carry-forward now treats a route-order-set route's **planned order as authoritative** (tier 0, source `planned_set`) over the driven order — so the office's arranging effort this year becomes next year's starting order. Field created via `web/scripts/setup_route_order_set_field.php` (ECK/field skip cim; sync YAMLs carry local UUIDs). **Known follow-ups:** merged-Mondays (last year's Fri+Sat both roll to Mon) may need a per-route Optimize; geocode the no-location stops; get the 16 new customers completed this year so they gain prior-year history. Docs owed: `__BOS_AI/Modules/route_editor.md` + ROADMAP row.

- **2026-08-30** — **Security updates — Drupal core 10.6.15 + dompdf/phpspreadsheet/entity/entity_browser (LIVE, first update on the new VPS).** Cleared **13 of 19** `composer audit` advisories (6 affected packages → 1). Updated: **drupal/core 10.6.12 → 10.6.15**, **dompdf/dompdf 3.1.5 → 3.1.6** (SVG/BMP DoS + local-file-read CVEs), **phpoffice/phpspreadsheet 2.4.5 → 2.4.7** (high), **drupal/entity 1.6.0 → 1.8.0**, **drupal/entity_browser 2.15.0 → 2.17.0**, + rides-along Symfony 6.4.44 patch bumps. `entity_browser_update_8202` ran. **Held: `guzzlehttp/guzzle` (high, CVE-2026-69246 host-bypass)** — its tagged fix is **7.15.2**, but `drupal/core-recommended` **pins guzzle to 7.12.x** on the 10.6 line and only an untagged `7.12.x-dev` carries the backport, so there's no stable fix on our core line yet; revisit when a core release bundles a fixed guzzle tag (BOS exposure low — guzzle only calls known hosts: Anthropic/Google/WEX). **Held (non-security):** the AI suite (`ai` 1.2→1.4 — would risk the just-built invoice-vision), `paragraphs`, `smart_date`, `entity_print`. Also **stopped `drupal-scaffold` from managing `web/robots.txt`** (`extra.drupal-scaffold.file-mapping` = false) so the AI-crawler blocks survive composer runs (core scaffold had clobbered it). **Process:** local branch → `composer update` (targeted, `--with-dependencies`) → DDEV `updb`/`cr`/audit verify → commit (`f86be5b5`) → deploy to live: rsync `composer.json`/`.lock` → **maintenance mode ON** → `composer install --no-dev` (`COMPOSER_PROCESS_TIMEOUT=3000`) → `updb` → `cr` → **maintenance OFF**. Verified live: 10.6.15, bootstrap OK, fixed versions installed, robots.txt GPTBot block intact, site ~0.26s. **Deferred guzzle tracked** in `deferred_work.md`.

- **2026-08-30** — **SERVER MIGRATION — BOS moved to a dedicated Managed VPS (LIVE).** BOS (and the rest of Todd's "Grand Mesa Hosting" reseller sites) moved off an **overloaded shared reseller box** onto a **dedicated Hosting.com Managed VPS**. Trigger: the old shared node sat at **load ~35–45 for 24h+** (noisy neighbors — our account was ~idle), making the app crawl/hang. **NEW server:** IP **`65.181.121.20`**, hostname **`d20712.use2.stableserver.net`**, CloudLinux 9.7 / cPanel 134, **8 dedicated vCPU / 16 GB / 300 GB NVMe**, **MariaDB 11.4** (was 10.5), **PHP 8.3** (same), **composer on PATH at `/usr/local/bin/composer`** (old box needed `/opt/cpanel/composer/bin/composer`). Steady-state load ~0.5–1. **OLD server (retiring):** `68.66.226.93` / `az1-tr2.supercp.com`. **How:** first a **manual BOS pre-migration** (rsync custom code + `config/sync`, `composer install` to rebuild core/contrib/vendor, DB dump→import, settings.php DB override, docroot symlink `public_html → brookstone/web`, `sites/default/files` recreated writable) as a working **proof/preview**; then **Hosting.com's free Migration Team did a full cPanel account transfer** (all sites **+ email**), which **replaced the manual copy** and reset the account (my cPanel-added SSH key was wiped — the migrated **`id_ed25519` key still authenticates**). **DNS:** NS at **grandmesahosting.com** (Todd's own reseller brand), A → `65.181.121.20`, **valid Let's Encrypt SSL**. **Files unchanged** (all on S3; `s3fs.use_s3_for_public = TRUE`; ~116.6k objects). **Verified:** **no data gap** (old==new: 48,763 WOs / 33,441 time-clock, identical max IDs — weekend timing meant nothing was written during the cut), bootstrap + DB + all custom modules + AI vision + S3 + cron (WEX 07:00/07:15, backup 02:30, active on new only — old crontab cleared) + WEX IMAP fetch + server-side geocoding all working. **Secrets rotated + verified** post-move (all had been exposed in-session): **s3fs AWS key** (settings.php; S3 write test), **DB-backup AWS key** (`~/.bos_s3_backup.env`; 149 MB upload test), **Anthropic API key** (settings.php putenv; live Claude call), **WO-intake key** (`BOS_WO_INTAKE_API_KEY`; unused endpoint, regenerated server-side). **Google geocoder key:** new IP `65.181.121.20` added to the IP allowlist. **Gotchas for future work on the new box:** (1) `sites/default` is `555` (hardened) — `chmod u+w` to edit settings.php, then re-harden; (2) app tree must be group-readable by the web server — the manual pre-migration needed `chmod -R o+rX` (LiteSpeed served **404** until the docroot tree was traversable by `nobody`); the full cPanel transfer handles this natively; (3) composer's default 300s process-timeout tripped on a large unzip during the manual rebuild — use `COMPOSER_PROCESS_TIMEOUT=3000`. **Follow-ups (Todd):** verify the other 3 reseller sites + email; keep old box ~1 week as rollback (flip A record back), then cancel the Grand Mesa reseller; delete the deactivated old AWS/Anthropic keys. **Deploy model unchanged** (rsync via the `brookstone` SSH alias, now → `65.181.121.20`; live drush = `/opt/alt/php83/usr/bin/php -d memory_limit=… vendor/drush/drush/drush.php`). Memory: [reference_live_server], [reference_new_server_migration].

- **2026-08-28** — **Invoice-photo → material-list import via AI vision (Claude) — SHIPPED LIVE.** New **third intake** on the WO material-list Import Items page (beside file-upload + paste): **snap/upload a photo of a supplier invoice/ticket** → a vision model reads the line items → they flow into the **existing** preview → match → confirm → import → learn pipeline (the `2026-08-28` Import Items feature). New **`InvoiceVisionExtractor`** service (`wo_material_list_management.invoice_vision`) sends the image to the `ai` module's **`chat_with_image_vision`** operation and returns rows (`identifier`/`description`/`quantity`/`unit_cost`/`uom`) + `vendor` + `document_type` + `warnings`. **Provider = Claude** — added **`drupal/ai_provider_anthropic`** (composer), model **claude-sonnet-4-5**, set as the vision default in `ai.settings`; extractor is **provider-agnostic** (swap to gpt-4o = config only). API key via the **`anthropic_api_key`** key entity (env provider → `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY`; kept out of git via `config_ignore`; **live value in `settings.php` `putenv`**, off-git). **Graceful gate:** the photo option only renders when `isAvailable()` (vision provider set **and** key resolves) — no dead button when unfunded (both `OPENAI_API_KEY` + before-key states verified hidden). **Two wins baked in:** the vision step **reads the UOM column** a CSV often lacks → preview **auto-flags case-priced lines** (the trap that garbled a SiteOne list), and flags **return/credit** tickets (negative qty). **Preview stays mandatory** — proven on a real sideways, cluttered DBC *return* ticket: extracted vendor + all 3 lines + UOM in ~6.7s via Claude, correctly flagged the return, and (as expected on a messy photo) got one sign + one part-number digit wrong → exactly what the confirm step catches. Setup per env (idempotent, no cim): `web/scripts/setup_anthropic_vision.php`. **Deploy:** rsync module + `composer.json`/`.lock` → live `composer install --no-dev` (installs the Anthropic provider) → `drush en ai_provider_anthropic` → run setup script → append `putenv('ANTHROPIC_API_KEY=…')` to live `settings.php` → `cr`. Roadmap row promoted from idea → shipped. Docs: `Modules/wo_material_list_import.md`.

- **2026-08-28** — **Material-list import round-trip bug fixed + corrupted live list repaired + toolbar reformat (`wo_material_list_management`, LIVE).** Same-day follow-up to the Import Items feature below. The office **exported** a material list from dev and **re-imported** it into live; **all totals came out wildly wrong** (a $0.88 coupling billed at **$429,010**). **Root cause = a `\b` word-boundary CSV-header bug:** the export header is `identifier, material_name, supplier_item_number, quantity, unit_cost`, but `normalizeTable` detected columns with `preg_match('/\b(cost|price|unit)\b/', …)` — and since `_` is a PCRE *word* char, `\bunit\b`/`\bcost\b` never matched `unit_cost` (nor `\bitem\b` on `supplier_item_number`, nor `\bmaterial\b` on `material_name`). `unit_cost` silently fell back to its **default column index 2 = the supplier_item_number column**, so every line's cost was read from the SKU (`429-010` → `$429,010`, `810080` → `$810,080`). Materials + quantities imported correctly; **only `field_material_cost` was garbage.** **Fix:** header detection now runs an **exact known-name pass first** (canonicalize the cell — punctuation/underscores → spaces — then map; our export + template headers + common vendor headers like `product id`/`your price`), falling back to the fuzzy `\b` matcher only when nothing is recognized — so an export now **round-trips**. Verified against the actual CSV in DDEV (36/36 rows read the correct costs, 0 suspicious). **Live data repair:** identified the one affected list (**6070**, WO **50684**, still Scheduled/1091) via a CSV-driven one-off (`web/scripts/fix_import_costs.php`) that resets `field_material_cost` **only** on lines whose stored cost equals the SKU-stripped-to-digits value — unambiguously the bad import, leaving all other lists untouched; **36 lines fixed**, line subtotals now sum to **$2,223.29** (markup $2,890.30). Every other list reported "0 garbage" (no collateral). WO 50684 not yet Complete, so its billing rollup will compute from the now-correct subtotals at sign-off. **Also (user-requested):** reformatted the material-list **action toolbar** — Add Item (primary) · Clone Items (secondary) · **Import / Export ▾** `dropbutton` (Import Items + Import Template + Export Items folded in) · Delete Items (destructive, `button--danger`, right-aligned) via new `css/actions.css` + `wo_material_list_management/actions` library. **Deploy:** surgical rsync of the fixed service to live + `cr` first (money bug), then the full module (form/css/libraries) + `cr`; no cim/DB migration. New gotcha in `drupal_bos_gotchas.md` ("`\b` word boundaries fail on `snake_case` headers"; round-trip your own exporter through your own importer). Docs: `Modules/wo_material_list_import.md`.

- **2026-08-28** — **WO material list "Import Items" — vendor-invoice import + catalog learning (LIVE).** New **Import Items** button (beside Clone Items) on a WO material list → a **full-page** flow (`/wo_material_list/{id}/import`): upload **CSV/XLSX** or paste rows → preview → import. Auto-detects columns (a SiteOne order CSV works as-is: **Product ID → identifier**, **Quantity**, **Your Price → unit cost**; ignores Retail/Total/Branch/On-Hand). **Matching:** material ID → supplier item # (`material_suppliers`) → **size-aware description fuzzy**. The fuzzy parses the nominal size to a decimal (**1-1/2 → 1.5**, 1/2 → 0.5, 3/4 → 0.75) and narrows candidates by the exact size string as BOS stores it ("1-1/2 in.") — because "1/2 in." is a substring of "1-1/2 in.", a token-only query mis-picked 1/2 for 1-1/2. **Preview** shows Item # · **Description** · status · a material autocomplete (pre-filled) · qty · cost; unmatched rows are mapped or skipped in place — never auto-creates a material. **Supplier "learn":** pick the vendor (e.g. SiteOne) + "remember item numbers & update prices" → on import, upsert the `material_suppliers` link (SKU + `field_supplier_unit_cost`) via find-or-update (the `material_supplier` module normalizes SKUs + blocks dup material+supplier links), so **future imports auto-match and catalog prices refresh from real invoices**. **Cost** = file price if given, else the existing presave fills from `material.field_cost_integer`; **duplicates merge** quantities; re-import is idempotent. Plus **Export Items** (re-importable CSV) + **Import Template**. Files: `MaterialListImportService` (parse/match/import/upsert), `ImportItemsModalForm` (full-page multi-step), `MaterialListExportController`. **Two design lessons baked in:** the multi-step belongs on a **full page**, not a modal (cramped table + flaky in-modal AJAX); and injected services in a **cacheable** form (managed_file) must be **declared, not constructor-promoted** — `DependencySerializationTrait` can't re-inject promoted props on unserialize (gotcha in `Governance/drupal_bos_gotchas.md`, which also caught a latent bug in `CreateCustomerForm`/`HandbookAcknowledgmentForm`). **Deploy:** rsync module + `cr` (no cim/DB). Verified live (routes, service, size-aware match). Pairs with the roadmap SiteOne ingestion — the office builds that catalog from real jobs. Docs: `Modules/wo_material_list_import.md`.

- **2026-08-27** — **New-customer intake: guided "Create Customer & Property" + full-name capture + server-side geocoding (LIVE).** Closes the loop from a public winterize signup to a real customer. **(1) Winterize forms** now capture **First + Last name** (stored as the full name in `field_submitted_name`; the property matcher still keys on the surname) and reword "Anything changed since last year?" → "Anything we should know about your sprinkler system?" on both public signup forms (new customers have no prior year); Bear Creek deadline → "Sign up by September 30th". **(2) Guided flow** (`bos_service_request`): a **"Create Customer & Property"** dropbutton op on any Needs-Review request **with no matched property** → `CreateCustomerForm` (prefilled from the request) with a **"Look up on Google"** geocode/confirm step; on submit `CustomerProvisioningService::provision()` creates, in ONE transaction, **property + phone_number + contact + client user (`client` role, username = the person's real name "First Last", NOT email) + customer_profile (`field_client_type` req; primary contact linked) + ownership_record**, then links the property back onto the request so the existing **Approve & Create WO** finishes. **(3) Google address search** enabled on the property map widget (`enable_property_address_geocoder.php`). **Two-key reality (gotcha):** `geofield_map` geocodes **client-side** (uses the browser/**referrer**-restricted key — the property map + its search work with the existing key), while the **`geocoder` MODULE geocodes server-side** and needs an **IP-restricted (or unrestricted) key with the Geocoding API** — Google refuses a referrer key server-side. Created a 2nd Google key IP-locked to the live server (`68.66.226.93`) + Geocoding-API-only, set it on the **`geocoder.geocoder_provider.googlemaps`** apiKey in **live active config only (not git)**, and added that provider to **`config_ignore`** so a cim can't clobber it. Verified live: real Montrose/Delta addresses geocode from the server. **Deploy:** rsync `bos_service_request` + run `enable_property_address_geocoder.php` + `cr` (no cim/DB). Dev testing note: the server key is live-IP-only, so "Look up on Google" degrades gracefully on dev (provisioning still works); dev also uses Google's **test reCAPTCHA keys** (active-only) so the public form is submittable there. Docs: `Modules/bos_service_request.md`, gotcha in `Governance/drupal_bos_gotchas.md`.

- **2026-08-26** — **Online Handbook Acknowledgment — `bos_handbook_ack` (LIVE).** Staff record a durable, auditable "I have read and agree to the Team Handbook" online — the digital counterpart to the printed wet-ink signature page (built by the 2nd Brain). **Append-only ECK entity `handbook_acknowledgment`** (bundle `acknowledgment`; fields `field_user`, `field_acknowledged_on`, `field_handbook_version`, `field_typed_name`, `field_ip`) — one record per event; `bos_handbook_ack_entity_presave()` throws on any edit of an existing record (legal integrity, all paths). **Versioning:** `field_handbook_version` on the handbook **cover**; the ROOT "Team Handbook" cover holds the current version (`HandbookAckService::currentVersion()`), snapshotted onto each ack; **bump it to require re-acknowledgment** (prior records retained) — keep it in step with the printed copy (ties to the handbook↔print alignment invariant). **UX:** a form block (staff-gated, `content_below` + request_path) on `/teammates/training/handbook/acknowledgment` — type-your-name e-signature + "I have read and agree" → snapshots user+version+timestamp+IP; shows "✓ already acknowledged on {date}" for the current version (no dup) and reappears only when the version bumps. **Report (Gate 4):** computed page `/admin/operations/training/handbook/acknowledgments` (admin menu under Handbook; perm `view handbook acknowledgments`) — acknowledged list + **version-aware GAP** ("not yet acknowledged") + version filter + completion %; computed not Views (version-aware gap isn't expressible in Views — same as Win-Back). **Audience: all staff** (`teammates`, `supervisor`, `administration`, `site_assistant`, `site_admin`, `administrator` = `STAFF_ROLES`) — 25 staff on live, not the 2500+ clients. Built via entity-API scripts (`setup_handbook_ack_entity.php`, `add_handbook_version_field.php`, `setup_handbook_ack_ui.php` — ECK/field configs skip on cim; block/menu are per-env content). **Deploy:** rsync module + scripts → `drush en` → 3 scripts → `cr` (no cim/DB). Verified live: entity + version seeded, 25 staff, 0 records at launch, form builds, block placed, report route live. **Gotcha:** a FormBase subclass can't re-declare `$requestStack`/`$currentUser` as promoted `readonly` props (parent already declares them) → fatal "Cannot redeclare … as readonly"; name injected props differently (`$ackSvc`, `$account`, `$reqStack`, `$dates`). Docs: `Modules/bos_handbook_ack.md`, `Entities/content_knowledge_entities.md`.

- **2026-08-26** — **Handbook Acknowledgment Log view (LIVE).** Companion to the status/gap report above: a Chat-requested Views audit trail `views.view.handbook_acknowledgments` at **`/admin/operations/handbook-acknowledgments`** (admin menu under **Handbook**, titled "Acknowledgment Log") — table over `handbook_acknowledgment_field_data`: staff member (linked) · signed name · version · acknowledged-on (short date) · IP; sortable, 50/page, newest-first; exposed filters version (contains) + acknowledged-on date range; total-count header; empty text. Added an **Employment** landing page (`site_landing_page:teammate`, `/teammates/employment`, `field_description` full_html) that features the Team Handbook and is a hub for future HR content (benefits, time-off, forms, safety), plus a **`teammate-navigation`** menu link ("Employment") to it. - **2026-08-25** — **Public/portal theme brand retheme — `brookstone_olivero` (LIVE).** Warm & immersive recolor of the public site + client portal to the Brookstone palette, matching the `/winterize` pages (admin/staff `brookstone_admin`/Claro left neutral by design). **Mechanism worth remembering:** Olivero derives its whole palette from a few HSL vars, and the **PRIMARY color is injected as an inline `style` on `<html>`** by `olivero.theme` from the **`base_primary_color`** theme setting — an inline element style beats any stylesheet `:root`, so CSS **cannot** override primary; you must set the theme setting. Set `base_primary_color = #CB6015` (brand orange; Olivero computes/injects hue 25) via **`drush cset brookstone_olivero.settings base_primary_color '#CB6015'`** (surgical, **not** cim). Everything else via one override file **`css/bo-brand.css`** (loaded last in the subtheme `global` library): warmed neutral grays (`--color--gray-hue:30`, `--color--gray-saturation:12%` → warm paper/ink), `--color--green → #007A33`, paper page background, footer pinned to flat brand **ink** with orange link hover, headings → **Oswald** + body → **Source Serif 4** (Google Fonts via the global library; form fields + primary nav kept sans). Also **flipped Olivero's backwards resting/hover**: body links, primary-menu links, and `.button--primary` now **rest** at bright `#CB6015` (primary-50) and **darken** on hover (Olivero ships resting = darker primary-40). `wo_clock` Clock In button `#2e7d32 → #007A33`. **Deploy:** rsync `brookstone_olivero` theme + `wo_clock` module, `drush cset` the primary color, `cr` (no cim, no DB). Verified live: `<html>` emits `--color--primary-hue:25`; `bo-brand.css` + both fonts load. Owner-approved; revisit items noted for a later pass. Gotcha: `Governance/drupal_bos_gotchas.md` ("Olivero primary color is injected inline").

- **2026-08-24** — **Winterize Win-Back call list — `bos_winback` (LIVE) + read-only CSV script.** New Office-menu page at **`/admin/office/winterize/win-back`** listing **prior-season winterizing customers with no work order this year** (the re-book campaign). A **computed set-difference recomputed each load** (nothing stored): source = `work_order:sprinkler_winterizing` created prior-year Aug 15 → Dec 31; exclude any `sprinkler_winterizing` WO created ≥ current-year Jan 1 (property already covered); result = uncovered source properties, deduped to the latest source WO. Years derive from the request date so it carries forward each season. **Contact resolution follows the BOS model** (shared with `web/scripts/winterize_winback_list.php`): `property.uid` is the office author, **not** the customer — the customer is the **latest `ownership_record.field_property_owner` (client user) → `customer_profile.field_primary_contact_ref`** (after WO `field_contact` / property `field_primary_contact_ref`/`field_contacts`); **phone on a contact is a reference** (`contact.field_phone_number → phone_number → .field_phone_number.value`, fallback the client user's `profile_phone_numbers`); placeholder `@sewardslandscape.com` migration emails suppressed; city via `field_zipcode_reference → field_city` label. On live: **210 customers, 191 with a phone**, $19,490 billed last year. Each customer is a BOS status card with **📞 click-to-call**, **Create WO** (prefilled `sprinkler_winterizing` add form) and **Edit** (`/properties/{pid}/edit`) deep links, plus call-outcome buttons **Left msg / No answer / Not interested / Reset**. **Drop-off:** creating a winterizing WO removes them next load (auto-covered); **Not interested** removes + suppresses; other outcomes annotate the card ("Left message · {caller} · {date}") so callers avoid double-calling. Call state persists in KeyValue `bos_winback.call_state` keyed `year:pid`; recorded via `POST …/mark/{property}` (AJAX, `_csrf_request_header_token`). Read-only over operational data — creates/edits no WO/property/contact; only the call-state records are written. **Deploy:** rsync module + `drush en bos_winback` (install hook grants `access winterize winback` to office roles) + `cr` (no cim/DB). The earlier **read-only CSV diagnostic** `web/scripts/winterize_winback_list.php` (652 source / 461 target / 211 win-back reconciled on live) is committed too (CSV output stays in `/tmp`, never tracked). Docs: `Modules/bos_winback.md`.

- **2026-08-23** — **Bear Creek $60 promo landing page + campaign variants (LIVE).** Added campaign variants **`bearcreek26`** (Bear Creek Subdivision) and **`online26`** (online promotions) to the allowlist (config/install + `hook_update_10004`) and the **Campaign QRs** page (renamed from "Postcard QR"; now lists every variant in a summary table — destination URL per code — plus printable QR blocks). New dedicated **`/winterize/bear-creek`** route reuses the signup `WinterizeForm` + intake with Bear-Creek copy and a **$60 offer** (regularly $90, residential up to 5 zones) in a hero offer block; the route **force-tags** requests `bearcreek26` (`resolveCampaign` route default). **$60 is advertised copy only** — no pricing/payment on the public page; the office applies the promo at billing via the campaign tag (intake stays pure intake). Marketing template is now route-aware (`bo_eyebrow/headline/subhead/cta_text/formband_*/offer/show_detail`) across signup / check-week / bear-creek; `ServiceRequestQrController` routes `pc26a`→check-week, `bearcreek26`→bear-creek, others→signup. Deploy: rsync module + scp template + `updb` (10004) + `cr` (no cim/DB migration). Verifier 31/31. Docs: `Modules/bos_service_request.md`.

- **2026-08-23** — **`/winterize/week` "Check your service week" — pc26a postcard (LIVE).** The **pc26a** ("already on our list") QR now lands on a dedicated **Check-your-week** page (`CheckWeekForm`, route `bos_service_request.check_week`) instead of the signup form; pc26b/website still go to `/winterize`. It **reveals a customer's scheduled week ONLY on contact corroboration** — the visitor enters last name + service address + ZIP + phone/email, and the week shows only when the phone/email **matches the matched property's on-file contact** (`ServiceRequestPropertyMatcher::contactCorroborates` — the same §6.0 gate as intake). A street-address-only or non-corroborated attempt returns an **identical neutral answer** (no enumeration); reCAPTCHA + a per-IP flood cap (`check_week_ip`, 15/hr, registered every attempt) guard against brute-forcing contact combos; no property element is ever rendered. The week is read from the WO's `scheduling` record (Monday of that week, `D m/d/Y`); "on the list, week not set yet" when the WO exists but is unscheduled; a "not found → sign up" CTA otherwise. The marketing template is now route-aware (parameterized `bo_eyebrow/headline/subhead/cta_text/formband_*` + `bo_show_detail` — signup shows the "what happens next" + accordions; the check page hides them). `ServiceRequestQrController` routes pc26a → check page. **Owner-accepted tradeoff (2026-08-23):** someone who knows a customer's address AND phone/email can see that customer's week — chosen deliberately over "call the office to confirm." Deploy: rsync module + scp template + `cr` (no cim/DB). Verifier `verify_service_request_phase2.php` **31/31 dev + live** (incl. check page renders, no property element, no week on GET). Docs: `Modules/bos_service_request.md`.

- **2026-08-23** — **BOS brand tokens published site-wide (LIVE).** Made the Brookstone palette (`--bo-*` CSS custom properties in `bos_service_request/css/bo-tokens.css`) the **single source of truth**, attached to every page (both themes) via `bos_service_request_page_attachments()` → `brand_tokens` library. **Survey finding:** the site's common CSS does **not** use the brand colors — the palette was new to `/winterize`, and the only oranges elsewhere are intentional **status** colors (a `#f5a623` alert border, the `#d35400` COD badge), not the brand accent — so there was nothing to "convert" and **no site recolor was done** (defining custom properties is inert until referenced). Tokens = CorelDraw Pantone accents (orange **#CB6015**, green **#007A33**, blue #62B5E5) + warm neutrals. New BOS convention: reference `--bo-*` instead of hardcoding brand hexes. Verified live: `bo-tokens.css` loads on front/admin/winterize; winterize verifier 27/27. (If BOS ever wants the public/admin UI to visually **adopt** the brand accents, that's a separate, review-gated design pass — not done here.) Deploy: rsync module + `cr` (no cim/DB).

- **2026-08-23** — **`/winterize` public landing page — Phase 3 presentation (LIVE).** Rebuilt the public winterize page to the approved Claude design (artifact fetched via WebFetch, artboard extracted from the `appifact-doc` block). **Dedicated marketing template** `page--winterize.html.twig` (theme-suggestion via `bos_service_request` `hook_theme_suggestions_page_alter` + `hook_preprocess_page`) renders **marketing chrome only** — logo/wordmark/tagline/phone, hero, form, dark footer — and **none of the site's nav/menus/regions** (verified 0 `<nav>`, no internal paths for anon; fixes the P3.1 leak). **Design:** Oswald (display) + Source Serif 4 (body); brand tokens in `css/bo-tokens.css` = **CorelDraw Pantone** accents (orange **#CB6015** 159 C, green **#007A33** 356 C, blue #62B5E5) + mockup neutrals (paper #FAF7F2, sand #F2E9DC, card #FFFDFA, ink #1A1613); page CSS `css/bo-winterize.css`. **Hero:** the owner's original background asset (CMYK→sRGB, downscaled to 1840×1026), **left-justified** `object-fit:cover` so the whole composition shows and fills top-to-bottom, with an **eased CSS fade** (50%→82%) layered over the softened printed fade + white behind the copy; eyebrow, headline, subhead, orange CTA, and 30+ years / Guaranteed stats. **Form card** on the sand band: 2-col grid, "Not sure"-first water-supply select, orange-ruled specific-date block, opt-in pair, disclaimer w/ warning glyph (`Markup::create` so the SVG survives), orange **"Get on the list"** submit (white text forced past Olivero's `.button`), 3-col "What happens next" w/ green icons, 2-col accordions (editable term body, JS-free `+`→`×`). **reCAPTCHA** replaces the math captcha (keys live). **JS** (`js/bo-winterize.js`, progressive): hero CTA drops the cursor into Last name; after a submit the page scrolls to the warning/confirmation (form sits below the hero). Logo trust mark is the transparent round emblem direct on the dark footer (no backing plate). Assets live **in the module** (`assets/`, deployed via rsync — not S3). Deploy: rsync module + scp template + `cr` (no cim/DB; the P0–P1/P3.3 field+view+config changes shipped earlier). Verifier `verify_service_request_phase2.php` **27/27 dev + live**. Owed: site-wide color-token refactor (palette now locked); P3.2b inline seasonal photos (not supplied). Docs: `Modules/bos_service_request.md`.

- **2026-08-22** — **Winterize signup open/close gate → Business Settings (LIVE).** The public `/winterize` intake gate had no admin UI (drush/config only). Moved it onto the **Business Settings** page (`config_pages:business_setting`, `/admin/config/business_settings`) as a new **"Public Service Requests"** group with 3 office-editable fields: **`field_winterize_signup_open`** (boolean master toggle), **`field_winterize_open_from`** / **`field_winterize_open_until`** (date-only window). `WinterizeForm::signupOpen()` now reads these config_pages fields first, **falling back** to the module config (`bundles.sprinkler_winterizing.{signup_open,open_from,open_until}`) when absent. - **2026-08-22** — **Services taxonomy — public vs teammate service pages (LIVE).** Each `/services/{name}` page renders **by the viewer's role, same URL**: public/clients see a "what we do" page; crew/office see a "how we do it" training page. New **`bos_services`** module — `hook_entity_view_mode_alter` switches the canonical full-page render to the **`teammate_view`** view mode for internal roles (`teammates`, `supervisor`, `administration`, `site_assistant`, `site_admin`, `administrator`), else **`full`** (public). **Two DEDICATED fields** (not shared — the summary lives on the public side, and no misleading names): **`field_service_public_desc`** (`text_with_summary`, **has the optional summary**) = public "what we do", shown in `full` + trimmed (300) as the `/services` listing teaser; **`field_service_crew_desc`** (`text_long`, no summary) = crew "how we do it", shown in `teammate_view` only. **`full` display** = banner image(s) (`field_banner_image`, multi-value) + iconic image + subtitle + public desc; **`teammate_view`** = crew desc + department + service code (no images — deliberate). **Form:** core taxonomy `description` retired from the form; **Public View** tab holds the public desc (summary widget), new **Teammate View** tab holds the crew desc (plain). Built via one idempotent entity-API script `web/scripts/configure_services_descriptions.php` — **no cim**; module is one `hook_entity_view_mode_alter`. Crew field **not rendered at all** for anon (no leakage — verified by term-page render + `/services` listing text scan). **Iteration note:** a first pass reused the shared `field_public_description` (text_long, no summary) for public + relabeled the shared `field_description` for crew; replaced same-day with the two dedicated fields above (content migrated, shared instances **removed from the services bundle**; their storages remain for equipment_types/material_types/backflow_*) so the public field is summary-capable and nothing is misnamed. Deployed dev → verified → live (safety dumps `~/pre-services-split-20260822.sql.gz.gz` + `~/pre-services-fields-redo-20260822.sql.gz.gz`; rsync module + scp script + run + `cr`; no cim/DB migration). ⚠ **SOP flagged** `CRW-SVC-TRN-001` (per-service crew training on the Services page) — logged in the **Pending SOPs backlog** in `SOPs/SOP-AUTHORING-WORKFLOW.md`; Chat authors. Docs: `Entities/services.md`, `Entities/taxonomy_vocabularies.md`.

- **2026-08-21** — **Winterizing schedule carry-forward — `bos:winterize:*` (SHIPPED LIVE; 457 records applied).** Two Drush commands in **`bos_scheduling`** that propose next season's `sprinkler_winterizing` schedule from prior-season history and, on a second explicit run, apply the reviewed proposals as real `scheduling:work_order` records. **Creates ONLY scheduling records + flips `work_order.field_scheduled`** — never creates/cancels/deletes a WO/Property/Contract, never writes `field_status` (that's `wo_schedule` via `wo_status_updates`). **`plan`** (read-only, safe on live) selects target-year winterizing WOs (created in the **calendar year**) with no scheduling record + not excluded status (1098/1097/1283/1281/1504); per candidate: prior counterpart **recency-wins never averaged** (source-years fallback chain 2025,2024; authority = the prior scheduling record's `field_date`), **date = same nth-weekday-of-month** (5th→last fallback, all via `DrupalDateTime`, never `FROM_UNIXTIME`), **tech = the ACTUAL signer** (`wo_complete_info.field_signed_off_by`, fallback planned assignee; dead/inactive → blank/unassigned), **route order = the driven order** (sign-off ts → clock start → Complete status ts → planned; dense 1..N per date+tech). Action split: **schedule** (confident + dead-tech-unassigned) / **review** (Sunday/closure only — holidays non-blocking, crews work 10/12) / **skip** (new customer, no prior + no neighbour). **Proximity fill:** new-customer rows placed on the nearest confidently-scheduled property's day (haversine over `field_geofield` ≤10mi), unassigned, flagged. Writes a full CSV + a focused `*_REVIEW.csv`. **`apply`** re-reads the reviewed CSV (the edited file is authority — does NOT recompute), re-validates each row against live, creates via the shared writer; `field_scheduled_firm=FALSE` (soft proposals) + `field_notify_assigned_teammate=FALSE`; **idempotent** (a WO with a schedule is skipped → a 2nd run is a no-op); one bad row never aborts. `--actor` required + switched-to for real access/attribution; **uid 1 rejected unless `--allow-superuser`** (uid 1 = Todd Wellman, who owned this run). New **`bos_scheduling.schedule_writer`** (`ScheduleWriter`) = the single scheduling-creation path, **extracted verbatim** from `SprinklerSchedulingController::save()` (bulk scheduler refactored onto it, behavior byte-identical); writes `field_date` (duration 1439 all-day) only — `wo_schedule`'s presave Smart-Date→DateRange sync back-fills legacy `field_scheduled_date_and_time` + `custom_date_all_day` sets the flag (verified, never hand-written). **§0.5 read-only probe** (`web/scripts/probe_winterize_order_signal.php`) chose the order+tech signals from 2025 data (634 sign-offs all irrigation_crew; sign-off ts real-time not batch; 23% planned-vs-actual tech mismatch → use actual signer). **Verifier** `web/scripts/verify_winterize_carry_forward.php` (8 checks: ≤1 schedule/WO, in-window+1439, field_scheduled flipped, nth-weekday rule, no excluded-status, dense route order, UI 200s). **Dev dress rehearsal** (DB synced from live): plan 459 → apply 457 → **8/8**; surfaced + fixed a verifier-only bug (its WO window started Aug 1, dropping a Feb-created 2026 WO → false dense-order FAIL; widened to calendar-year to match `plan()`). **Live:** safety dump (`~/pre-winterize-apply-20260821.sql.gz.gz`, 132M, verified) → plan 459 (432 auto+tech / 25 unassigned [18 proximity new-customers + 7 dead-tech] / 2 Sunday-review / 0 skip) → apply `--limit=10` (8/8) → full apply **447 (+10 = 457), 12 skipped** (10 already + 2 review) → **8/8, 457 records** → idempotent re-run 0/459. Distribution Sep 33 · Oct 421 · Nov 3 (Mon 10/12 Columbus Day carries 19, non-blocking). **Office follow-ups:** confirm/assign the 25 unassigned + manually add the 2 Sunday rows, from `~/tmp/winterize_plan_2026_REVIEW.csv` on live. Deploy: rsync `bos_scheduling` + scp verifier + `cr` (no cim/DB). Docs: `__BOS_AI/Modules/winterize_carry_forward.md`.

- **2026-08-20** — **Public service-request intake layer — Gates 1–4 (LIVE).** New **`bos_service_request`** module + **`service_request`** ECK entity (bundle `sprinkler_winterizing`, 23 fields). A public, unauthenticated path that produces a **controlled internal request record** and creates a real Work Order **only after office approval** — intake is never execution; no anonymous path creates/schedules a WO/Property/Contact. **Gate 1** — entity/bundle/fields (via idempotent `setup_service_request_entity.php`, not cim), `service_request_status` vocab (New/Needs Review/Verified/Already Covered/Duplicate/Rejected/Converted — content, resolved by name; TIDs differ dev/live), perm `administer service requests`. **Gate 2** — services: `ServiceRequestEligibility` (first-hit authority: property `field_no_services` → owner `field_credit_hold`/`field_do_not_schedule` via latest ownership_record → existing non-Canceled WO in service-year window → **contract coverage** [`contract_sections` field_service=369 + `field_do_you_want IN (1 Yes, 4 Accepted)` on a current-year residential contract in status {1123,1651,1124,1125,1127}] → existing active request → eligible), `ServiceRequestPropertyMatcher` (server-side ZIP-filtered, suffix-agnostic street prefilter), `ServiceRequestConverter` (locked, transactional, idempotent; delegates WO creation to `bos_wo_intake.intake`), `ServiceRequestStatusResolver`. Extracted `bos_wo_intake`'s private normalizers into a shared **`PropertyMatchNormalizer`** service both modules use (live intake re-verified). **Gate 3** — public **`/winterize`** form (Form API, captcha, flood per-IP-hr + per-address-yr, campaign `?c=` allowlist, open/closed gate). **§6.0 property-disclosure invariant** enforced + tested vs rendered HTML: no property element/candidate, injected `property_id` ignored+logged, matching entirely server-side, confirmation byte-identical (ref aside) for matched/ambiguous/unmatched; unmatched creates ZERO properties/contacts; "already on our list"/duplicate copy fires ONLY on email/phone contact corroboration (enumeration control). **Gate 4** — office queue view **`service_request_admin`** at **`/admin/office/service-requests`** (built by `build_service_request_admin_view.php`); **Approve & Create Work Order** + Mark Duplicate / Already Covered / Reject as **per-row confirm forms** (NOT VBO — the documented VBO-footgun family); `hook_entity_operation` dropbutton; presave backstop blocks status→Converted without a linked WO. Verifiers `web/scripts/verify_service_request_gate{2,3,4}.php` (13/13, 13/13, 10/10 dev; read-only on live). **`/winterize` is OPEN on live** (`open_from` set to 2026-08-20 for preview; office phone 970-835-9661). **Deploy:** scp module + `drush en` + entity/vocab/view scripts + `cr` (no cim/DB). Remaining: Gate 5 (campaign report + QR asset), Gate 6 (more bundles, offseason). First workflow = 2026 winterizing postcard QR (mails early–mid Sept). §5.4 contract-status set broadened beyond the spec's three (added 1651 Generate WO + 1127 Completed) — flagged, easily narrowed. Docs owed: `__BOS_AI/Modules/bos_service_request.md` + ROADMAP row.

- **2026-08-16** — **Public menu: "Lighting" moved under the Services tab (LIVE + dev).** On the public site (`main` menu), the service links are `taxonomy_menu`-derived off the **services** vocabulary and **hand-arranged** (the menu doesn't purely mirror the taxonomy — e.g. "Holiday Decorations", a child of the Lighting term, sits under Services). "Lighting" (services term **1505**) had been left at the **top level** instead of nested under the **Services** view link (`views_view:views.services.page_1`) like its siblings (Landscaping/Sprinklers/Snow/etc.). Fixed by setting that derived link's parent to the Services link via `MenuLinkManager::updateDefinition` — a **runtime menu override** (survives a full `menu.link` rebuild; verified), **NOT config/cim**. Idempotent script **`web/scripts/fix_lighting_menu_parent.php`** (run per env — menu overrides are DB/runtime, not deployed). Lighting keeps its own Landscape/Exterior Lighting children as a sub-menu under Services. A plain `->rebuild()` did **not** fix it (proving it was an override, not stale cache). Applied live + dev.

- **2026-08-16** — **Off-site DB backups → S3 (LIVE).** The nightly `bos_db_backup.sh` (2:30 AM, `drush sql:dump --gzip`, 14-day rotation in `~/db_backups`) now **also pushes each dump to S3** — a separate failure domain from the Hosting.com data center (motivated by the **2026-08-13/14 Phoenix AZ1 thermal outage**: storm knocked out DC cooling → network/power disruption → BOS unreachable ~1 hr; server never rebooted — 846-day uptime — it was facility-level, not our box, and **not** the concurrent photo-scan we initially/wrongly blamed). **New bucket** `brookstone-db-backups` (**us-east-1**, Global namespace, Block-all-public, SSE-S3, ACLs disabled, lifecycle **expire after 30 days**). **New least-privilege IAM user** `brookstone-db-backup` with an `s3:PutObject`-only policy scoped to `arn:aws:s3:::brookstone-db-backups/*` (**no read/delete/list** — a compromised box can only drop backups, not read data or wipe the archive; incomplete-multipart cleanup left to lifecycle). Upload uses **new `web/scripts/s3_backup_upload.php`** (single PutObject via the **aws-sdk-php already in `vendor/`** — s3fs dep; **no AWS CLI on the box**, none installed). Credentials live **off-git** in `$HOME/.bos_s3_backup.env` (chmod 600; `AWS_ACCESS_KEY_ID`/`AWS_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY`/`BOS_S3_BACKUP_BUCKET`/`BOS_S3_BACKUP_REGION`) sourced by the script; `.bos_s3_backup.env` gitignored. S3 failure **emails an alert but does NOT abort** the local dump (local stays primary). **No cron change** — the existing 2:30 cron calls the same script. Verified end-to-end on live (dump → `uploaded s3://brookstone-db-backups/…` → rotation kept 14); also pulled a manual post-incident dump off-server to `/mnt/d/Backups/brookstone-live/` (gzip-verified). ⚠ Access key passed through chat during setup — **rotate it** (IAM → user → Security credentials). Deploy: `scp` the 2 scripts + create the env file; no rsync/cim/DB.

Deployed dev → verified property→WO copy → live (**no maintenance mode, zero downtime** — crews were mowing). Docs: `Entities/property_detail_entities.md`.
